[1\verso] @ namaḥś caṇḍavajrapāṇaye ||
athāto bhagavataḥ Vipulaprabhāsaṃ nāma nagare viharati sma || ihatā devanāgayakṣarākṣasāsuragaruḍakinnaramahogaiḥ manuṣyāmanuṣyaiḥ mahatā riṣibhiḥ mahatā satvanikāyasamāgate mahādharmadeśanākathām akā+ṣīt ||
% the scribe is very thick... mistaking makāra for initial ikāra!
atha khalu tatra parṣadamaddhye Vijayaprabhāso nāma sre[2]ṣṭī dhanakanakasamṛddha yāvad vaisravaṇapratispardhī vaidehīsaṅgataḥ mahān upāsakaḥ srāddhaḥ kalyāṇāśraya sa ca rājā Caṇḍapradyotenopadrutaḥ vadhake jñaptaḥ sa bhītas tatro bha<ga>vatas sakāṣam upāgataḥ |
atha Vijayaprabhāśaḥ śreṣṭīḥ upāśakāḥ utthāyāsanāt || bhagavataḥ purataḥ karuṇakaruṇaṃ paridevamāṇaḥ pa[3]rudan evam āha ||
bhagavan mama rājā roṣābhibhūtaḥ vadhakebhya ājñāptaḥ tenāhaṃ jīvitahetor atīva bhayāmi || kathaṃ mayā bhagavan karttavyaṃ kathaṃ pratipattavyaṃ bhagavan mama trāṇau bhavasva parāyaṇāṃ ||
atha khalu Sākyamunir bhagavān Vijayaprabhāsaṃ samāsvāsya mahārasmayaḥ ūrṇākosād vinirgatāḥ tri pradakṣiṇīkṛtya [4] bhagavato mukhadvāre praviṣṭāḥ samanantarapraviṣṭāś ca bhagavata lalāṭād udakaṃ yathā muktaphalaṃ śravitum āravadhvaḥ bhagavān dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇi prasārya lalāṭāṃ parimārjya dharaṇitalaṃ tāḍayaṃ samanantaratāḍitaṃ | dharaṇitalaṃ ṣaḍvikāraṃ prakaṃpitum ārabdhaḥ imāṃ dharaṇī bhitvā Vidyujvālākarālī mahārākṣaśī samudbhū[5]tā aṅgārarāsinayanā vidyu laṃbodarī daṃṣṭrākarālī kapilapiṃgalajaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍita nīlakāyā aṣṭādaśabhujā nānāpraharaṇadharā raudrāṃ caṃḍā dvādaśasiro naracarmavasanā kapālamālī majārudhiramedakapālapūrṇṇahastā jvālī siṃhacarmavasanī | kṛṣṇabhujaṅgayajñopavitā khaḍgaaṃkusacakramusaratri[2\recto]śūlaṃ paṭṭapatākālaguḍakuntatiryakśaktikapāladharaḥ dhanusarasaktidaṇḍapāsaparśudharāḥ sarvapraharaṇaprajvalitaḥhastāḥ || uccaskandhāḥ sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitā bhayaṃkarā bhagavataḥ purataḥ sthitvevam āha ||
% prob. vidyu = vidyujjihvā cf. with the first vidyā: [...] vidyujihvā mahodari [...]
vayaṃ brūhi
atha bhagavān Vidyujvālākarālī mahārākṣaśīrm evam āha ||
gaccha tvaṃ rākṣasī sarvapratyarthikapratyami[2]trāṃ sarvaduṣṭapraduṣṭāṃ pratinivārayaḥ sarvabaṃddhaḥ kalikalahaḍiṃbarumaraṇānābhayākyalā nivāraya |
atha Vidyujvālākarālī mahārākṣasī bhagavataḥ pādayor nipatyaḥ śirobhir abhivaṃdyaḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya bhagavantam etad avocat |
asti bhagavaṃ mama hṛdaya raudrajvālā nāmaḥ karālī sarvasatrupramathanī sarvayakṣarākṣa[3]sapisācāpasmārakaṭapūtananiṣedhanī sarvamārapramadranī tad ahaṃ bhagavataḥ purataḥ kīrttayāmi <|>
bhagavān uvāca
bhāṣasva mahārākṣasi || adheṣṭita mayā ||
atha rākṣaśī imāṃ vidyāṃ bhāyate smaḥ
namaḥs traiyadhikānāṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ | oṃ raudriṇi prakurvitacaṇḍamahāvegadhara vidyujihvā mahodari raudrī kapālamālā[4]dhara jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍita nānāpraharaṇadharaḥ hūṃḥ <2> sphaṭaḥ 2 tathāgatasamayam anusmara huru 2 <| || |> hūṃ pramardaya sarvasatrūṇāṃ śroṇitaṃ piba pracaṇḍavegadhara paca napa tāpaya hūṃ vikira 2 hana 2 hana 2 sarvasatrūṃ sarvaduṣṭrāṃ nikṛṃtaṃ <pra>sādaya hūṃ mahāvegadhara kālakā hūṃ phaṭ 2 amukaṃ me vasam ānaya<| ṃ |> śvāhā ||
% sphaṭaḥ prob. misreading phaṭ?
anena bhagavaṃ rau[5]drakarmāṇi karttavyāni |
gairikena mahārākṣasī likhya vastr<| ā |>e vā bhurjāpatre vā phalake vā guguludhūpa dahatā vidyām aṣṭaśatavārāṃ jape<t> || raktapuṣpāni dātavyāni smaśāne nikhaṃtavya | tasyopari sarṣapam aṣṭasatavārāṃ parijapya kṣep<| a |>tavyaṃ nāma ca gṛhītavyaṃ saha nikhanitamātreṇa sarvapratyarthikapratyamitrā duṣṭasa[2\verso]tvajaṃ bhitā stambhitāś ca bhaviṣyanti ||
smasānāṅgāreṇa kanyāpratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā bhage aṃguliṃ sthāpya japed aṣṭaśatavārāṃ vasā bhavanti ||
sinthakamayīpratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇārūpya aṣṭasahasraṃ japeta vasā bhavati ||
% or jape te
tām eva pratikṛtiṃ bhage karavīrapuṣpāṣṭasate + vā hattavyā vasā bhavati |
atha mātṛsthāne nikhane sarvasatra[2]vo mariṣyanti |
sapta rātreṇa rudhirabaliṃ nivedyaṃ sarvatra sarvaduṣṭāṃ paśū nivedyamāṇā maṃ<| s |>gṛhya dātavyaṃ <||> dvisaptāhena kulotsādanaṃ kariṣyati ||
tṛsaptā<he>na y<| r |>adi nāgacchati sagṛha || sagrāma sakudyaprakārā (?) vinaśyati <||>
mānusāsthimayaṃ satrupratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā rudhireṇa makṣrayet || nābhisthāne hrī<ḥ>kāraṃ nyaset || lalāṭe [3] oṃkāraṃ nyaset | dakṣiṇabāhu dhrīkāraṃ vāmabāhūṃ hūṃkāraṃ dakṣiṇapādapṛṣṭhe pāṭkāraṃ | vāmapādapṛṣṭe dhakāraṃ nyaset | tatpratikṛtiṃ gṛhyaḥ smasānacīrena veṣṭayet | smasāne nikhane tasyopari mūlamantreṇa sariṣapa aṣṭābhimantritaṃ amukaṃ māraya iti | kṣeptavyaṃ udhṛtya mokṣaḥ
% -bāhūṃ attracted by hūṃ-
% yāṭ- misreading of phaṭ
% -cīrena read -cīvareṇa
rājarkkasya vināyakaṃ pratimā kṛtvā ṣaḍa[4]ṅgulaṃ mastake vajrāṃ nyaset || ṣoḍaṣakṣe tṛsūlaṃ dantacarike parśu dakṣiṇabāhu dhrīḥkāraṃ vāmabāhuḥ dhrīkāraṃ dakṣiṇapādapṛṣṭe cakāraṃ vāmapādapṛṣṭe ikāraṃ bāhumadhyapṛṣṭe aṃkuśa vajrasya dakṣiṇe sūcyāgre rekha rudhirodbhavabudbadaḥ tasyā vāmasūcyā madhya<| e |>mas tathaiva tatpratikṛti gṛhyaḥ rudhiraplutaḥ ucitasaṃ[5]skārabhūmipradeśe nikhane tasyopari rudhiraplutaḥ sarṣapenāsū<| + |>ktābhimaṃtritaṃ tasyopari kṣeptavyaṃ ya icchati taṃ vaktavyaṃ yadi atikramiṣyati | vināyaka sākṣāt tasya gṛha svayam eva lālanajihvā sṛgālamārjjarasūkarava<| ¯ |>rāha<| ¯ |>kāka etaiḥ samastaiḥ vināyakasadṛsaiḥ gṛhyante nagararāṣṭrajanapadasa + [3\recto] mārī pravarttante na sarva svayaṃ niḥpratyānayanaṃ karttavyaṃ || ta<t>pratikṛtī uddhṛtya sarvanyāsaṃ kṣīreṇa prakṣālaye<t> gandhapuṣpadhūpadadhibhaktaṃ dātavyaṃ sukhasthāne sthātavyaṃ || mahāsāntir bhavati | guhyena karttavyaṃ mantravaraḥ ||
nibakāṣṭhai pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā satravaḥ tatpratikṛtiṃ raktacandanena samālabhe || nābhisthāne hrīḥkāraṃ nyaset | taṃ gṛ[2]hya  samudragāminīnadyā prakṣipet | amukaṃ māraya iti | tatpratikṛtiṃ punar api saṃskārayet | kṣīrapātre sthāpaye sāntir bhave ||
candragrahe sūryagrahe vā māṣā mukhe prakṣipyeḥ yāva sūryo grahamuktaḥ tasyā māṣāṅkuram upalakṣya prakṣālam api punar api gandhodakena prakṣālaye sucaiṣu karmābhirataiḥ || brāhmaṇagṛhe kanyā [3] sarvālaṃkāra maṃḍalakaṃ kṛtvā tanmaṇḍale pūrvābhimukhāṃ kanyāṃ sthitvā pūrvoktamāṣāṃ kanyāhastena pariniryātayet || tad dravyaṃ gṛhya guḍikāṃ kuryāt | gulikāṃ guhya cchāyā śuskāṃ kuryāḥ sapta gulikaṃ madhunā saha bhakṣayet || śrutidharo medhāvī balīpalitavarjitaḥ karṇṇikārasamaprabhaḥ dehaḥ
% kanyāṃ very odd anusvāra, more like a squiggle atop the next aṅkuśa
% guhya = gṛhya
taṃ guḍikāṃ vintiḥ (?) gṛhya suca[4]varṇarajaśaco (?) veṣṭayet | suprasastaghaṭaṃ gṛhya sthāne candanena mrakṣayitvā kanyākarttikasūtreṇa veṣṭayet | <ta>taḥ gulikā ghaṭo prakṣipya nacavastreṇa veṣṭayet | tada ghaṭaṃ citrāpayet gṛhya madhye nikhane dhanadhānyā kṣayaṃ na yāti pravarddha<| na |>mānaśrī bhavati

vasikartukāmena sinthakamayīṃ puttalīṃ kṛtvā triyā vā puruṣasya vā sravaṇabrāhmaṇa[5]rājaja + hānāya(?) grahaṇaṃ karttavyaḥ ekagulikāt tasya udadhe sthāpayitavyaṃ || sā ca pratikṛtiṃ prāṅmukhaṃ sarṣapasumukhabījaṃ vārāṣṭasahasraṃ parijapya sthāpayitavyaṃ tāmrabhājane trisadhyaṃ udakamiśreṇāṣṭaśatavārāṃ mantraṃ parija<| ṃ |>pya pratikṛtiṃ tāḍayet || guguludhūpaṃ dātavyaṃ ekarātrena vasya bhavati daśayojanābhyantareṇāka[3\verso]\{rṣano bhaviṣyati | dvirātraprayogena sā stri yada\} tradāreṇa mahāvaśā bhavati || trirātraprayogena śravaṇabrāhmaṇā vaṣyā bhaviṣyati || saptarātraprayogena rājāntaḥpuraparivāraḥ sadhanahiraṇyaṃ suvarṇṇena vasyo bhavati || dāsaddhaḥ yāvajīvan tiṣṭati || dvisaptāhaprayogena sarvabhayabandhanatāḍanatajaṃnyābhyākhyā(?)nādi\{bhiḥ\}[2]\{parimūddhata sarva ca strīpuruṣadāraka\}dārikādayo yata kiṃkarakā bhaviṣyanti || yadi sīghraṃ nāgacchanti tatas tadāhaṃ bhagavaṃ teṣām ekatocchvāsena sarvojāṃ harāmiḥ rudhiramajjaṃ pibāmiḥ || mahāsoṣaṃ kāye prakṣepsyāmi ||
% yata = yāva[t]?
sarvaduṣṭanigrahaṃ kattukāmena anena mama hṛdayena sarṣapa parijapya khadirakīlaṃ saptābhimantritena \{pacaraṃgi\}[3]\{kasūtrena veṣṭitakaṃ dvāre nikhattavyaṃ\} || \{salaṃghi\}tamātreṇa indriyabandhaḥ kṛto bhavati || jambhita saparivārasya bhavati | yāva rmedhṛyate  tāva indriyabandhaḥ kṛto bhavati | udhṛto mokṣaḥ
mānuṣāsthimayaṃ kīlaṃ ṣaḍaṅgulapramāṇa<| pa |> paṇcaraṅgikasūtreṇa veṣṭayet || saptābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā sarṣapamisreṇa kṣaṇāgataḥ striyāḥ ekīsthāne ha\{nta\}vyā || [4] + + + + + + + + \{((dissa)) hitaṃ bhavati\} || \{udhṛtya\} mokṣaḥ ||
mṛgasṛgālāsthikīlakaṃ caturaṃgulaṃ pramāṇaṃ <||> aṣṭasatābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā kṣīravṛkṣe tu smasānāṅgāreṇa kanyāpratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā bhagasthāne tatkilaṃ hantavyaṃ | saha haṇitamātreṇa mahatā jvareṇa gṛhyate || udhṛtya mokṣaḥ
vyāghrāsthimayaṃ kīlayaṃ ekāṃgulapramāṇaṃ kīlamadhye ṭhakā[5]\{ra nyase punar api vyāghracarmeṇa veṣṭayet\} || yasya nāmnā smasāne nikhane ni<khani>tamātreṇa krandāpayati vyāghradva<ḥ> udhṛtya mokṣaḥ

kapālarudhirasarṣapaiḥ + [5] + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + + para + + + + + + + + + + + + + + | striyā vā puruṣasya vā sarvajātimatā bhavati | sarṣapakṣīramantreṇāṣṭa + sata juhuyāt muktā bhavati |
kapālacūrṇṇaṃ aṣṭasahasravārāṃ parijapya saṣapavacākuṣṭhena saṃyojyaḥ vasūmūtrena (?) bhāva[4\verso]yet argada (?) gandhayet | puna aṣṭasahasraṃ parijapya nāmnā juhuyāt | ekaviṃsativārāṃ parijapya yojanābhyantareṇākarṣayati vasyā bhavaṃti <yā>vajjīvaṃ dāsabhūtā iva tiṣṭanti ||
dvāramūle nikhane laṅghitamātreṇa mahāvyādhinā gṛhyate kṣīra<| ā |>sarṣapena snāpaye mokṣo bhavati ||
sa eva guḍikāṃ pānabhojanena saha [2] dātavyaṃ dadyaḥ sadā bhūta iva tiṣṭati ||

aṣṭasahasrajaptena bāhau ba<| ta |>ndhayet || sarvasthānagatasya jayo bhavati | mahāvīryaparākramo bhavati | nihitasatrur bhavati | sarvasatva vaśagatā tiṣṭhaṃti || sarve va dāśabhūto bhaviṣyanti |
mukhe prakṣipet sarvakalikalahavigrahavivāda apratihatavādī bhavati ||
aṣṭa[3]satāṃ gulikāṃ raktavastreṇa veṣṭayet || pañcaraṃgikasūtreṇa badhvā buddhasya pādamūle nikhane aṣṭaśatavārā parijaptavyam || sarvasatvā dāsabhūtā bhaviṣyanti || sarvaduṣṭa sarvapratyarthikapratyamitraiḥ sarvayakṣarākṣasa<| ¯ |> mitrabhūtā bhaviṣyanti || ekaputravat || pratipālayanti |
guḍikāṃ ghṛtamisrāṃ draṣṭaśataṃ juhu[4]yāt || mantraṃ japtvā mahatā bhayā\{t\} parimucyate || rājam antaḥpuraparivārā vaso bhavati || ātmanena dhanena ca |
gugulumisreṇāṣṭasataṃ parijapya juhuyāt || yasya nāmnā sa dvidviṣṭo bhavati | rājābandhanagato bhaviṣyati ||
bālatuṣanarandatta | kuṣṭavacāgorocanarocanī caivaḥ akṣamayūra sasa ullakas ta[5]thā tatraiva yat kaściṃ (?) taṃ bhasmasahita ekīkṛtya āttadhūmena dehe || kaṭukataile madrayet | sithakalūkena sālyodanaṃ kapālasthitaṃ m-ekīkṛtyaḥ || śīrṣakasyā kecinta pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā raktacandanena rudhiraplutaṃ mrakṣayitvā acira sa<| ṃ |>skāretabhūmipradese dakṣiṇābhimukhasthita nikhaṃtavyaṃ ṣaḍmukhamudrāsahitaḥ [5\recto] hṛdayamantraṇaḥ tri pradakṣiṇāḥ vāmena parāṅmukhaḥ gantavyaḥ nātidūre dūragatam api vacanaṃ na śrūyate || tasyāpi upalakṣaṇavidhiḥ karttavyaṃ nivarttayitavyaṃ nivarttayituṃ udhṛtya mokṣaḥ |
citibhasma gṛhya saptasahasravārān parijapyaḥ mārge prakire yasya nāmnā kriyante saha laṃghitamātreṇa mahāvyādhinā [2] gṛhyate || satajapta pratyāyaṇaṃ |
\{tat\} bhasmaṃ gṛhya sarṣapamisreṇāṣṭaśa<| ta |>tābhimantritaṃ ekīsthāne nikhane | nikhanamātreṇa kosam | nindimakhaṇḍakhaṇḍā patati ||
mārjārakāsthimayī kīlakaṃ tṛyaṃgulapramāṇaṃ sarṣapamiśrenāṣṭasatābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā dvekīsthāne nikhane daśāṃgulaṃ kuru 2 kundāpayati | sarvaja[3]navidviṣṭo bhavati ||
bhallātakakāṣṭa gomahisasvānāsvagardabhamanuṣāsthi pataṃg<| u |>akusuṃbhacandanakuṃkumamadayantikākisukarocanā caiva siṃdūrahiṃgulu tathāṃjanamanarsilā caiva | kuryaviṣaṃ tathā | kanakasya pañcāṃgaiḥ etaiḥ samastaiḥ ekīkṛtyaḥ rudhireṇa mardayet || sakṛt śukāni cūrṇṇa gṛhya sarṣapamiśre[4]ṇāṣṭaśatābhimantritaṃ satrugṛhe<| ca |>ṣu ca dhūpayet | dhūpitamātreṇa mahatāt | sarapātramātram abhyantare mānuṣā mahatā śirorogo cakṣurogena ca pataṃti prākārakāṣṭhapāsāna suvarṇṇamayā dṛsyante kṣiti khanyante | unmattavad bhrāmanti | pratyāyana karttavyaṃ kṣīrasarṣapasalilamiśreṇa hṛdayamantreṇa aṣṭa[5]śatābhimantritaṃ satrugṛheṣu ca dhūpayet trikāla pāyayet tasmā vinasyamātre ṣaḍmukhamudrāmantrasahitaṃ pracchāditaṃ hastena daśadisaṃ vimārjjayet ||

atha sā mahārākṣasī bhagavantam idum uvāca<| ṃ |> |
ratnatrayāpakāriṇyaṃ upaghātāya st<| u |>ūpabhedakānā pratibiṃbabhedakānāṃ sadharmapratikṣepakānāṃ duṣṭasatvānā pai[5\verso]śūnyaasadbandhapralāpabhāṣāṇāṃ tatsadviṣānā satvānāṃ mahatocchvāsena krodhabhayabhairavarūpeṇa nigrahaṃ kariṣyāmi ||

buddhasya satyam atikramitānāṃ dharmasatyam atiḥkramitānāṃ saṃghasatyam atikramitānāṃ saṅghalābhaparihāṇakānāṃ | tadrudhiramānsamatsyameda bhakṣayiṣyāmi | ratnatrayābhiratapuruṣāṇāṃ upadrutavedakapuruṣaṃ yameyam atiyo[3]jitam api pādenāvaṣṭabhya jihvā gṛhṇāmi kiṃ mānuṣāṃ m-abhayo trāsayanti trayatriṃsadbhavaṇanivāsinām |

atha pañcamahābhautikī [7\verso] sānubhāvā prardarśayaṃ |
pranṛtyamāṇāṃjalyā hahaḥ | hūṃkāraghūrmita
nāditāvanatacūtayāḥ (?) dhyeṣitaṃ |
sriyaṃ vasyārtha siddhārtha abhicārārtha pauṣṭika ||
deśaya paramaṃ devi maṇḍalā pāramārthikaṃ |
ca<| tu |>turasra caturdvāra catustoraṇamaṇḍitaṃ
catusūtraparikṣiptam aṣṭastaṃbhāgrasaṃsthitam |

ekādasasirākāra<| ā |>ṃ saroṣa asitānanaṃ |

atha pañca<| pa |>mahābhautikī | sādhanapaṭalam adhyeṣayaṃti |
kṛṣṇa[3]pakṣe amāvāsyā devatī raktapañcamāṃ
raktaraktopacāreṇa raktamālyāvadhāriṇīm |
raktavasanaṃ dhyātvā raktapūjābalin dadet |
raktamālyakapālāś ca matsamāṃsā tathaiva ca ||
raktabhāṇḍa puro bhiveṣya pūjaye<t> āpayogataḥ
ayutaṃ tṛguṇaṃ japet takṣaṇā vaśam āṇayet |
saptāhenaiva niyatā ātmanena vāpāsu [4]
dattapalaṃ gṛhya dātmam evātmapāṇinā ||
śuddhapakṣe caturdasyāṃ nīlavarṇṇaṃ tu bhāvayet |
mahāmāṃsādi madyādi dāpaye rudhirāṇi ca |
% NB: offering of human flesh, alcohol, blood
yathāsakya bali tosya sā sidhyati ca devatī |
kṛṣṇapakṣe navamyāṃ tu pūjaye devatī sadā |
raktanīleṃduvarṇṇāni drutacañcaladṛṣṭavat |
rījayed eva yogena nirvikalpe[5]na dhīmatā
nānāprakārapuṣpāṇāṃ dhūpadīpasugandhayā
raktamedavaśamarjjamatsamāṃsāni pūjayet |
dāpaye caṭakāhāra vasakāś ca niveśayet |
pūjāmūle phalānāṃ tu pṛṣṭa pāni tathaiva ca
rudhiraṃ kuṃbhapūrṇṇa kṣīraṃ vāpi tathaiva ca |
pāyasaṃ kuṃbhapūrṇṇena dāpayet sva nivedayet |
tilagodhūma[11\recto]māsāś ca kusalatthavrīhisannidhau ||
svedāye dāpaye bhotiṃ matsamāṃsasamanvitā |
nairityā disi proktadi toṣaye<| o |> bhautiṃkā balim |
evaṃ kṛtvā ca pūjyaiś ca toṣyamānai<ḥ> sa sidhyati |
ya tu kāṃkṣate mantrī tatra sidhyanti yanyathā |
raktapiṇḍakhagādhiyā anupravṛtātvā raktakarmasu sidhyati |
saptāś ca niyuta japtā<| ś ca ni[2]yu |>ta japtā sidhyatehaiva janmani
% NB: siddhi in this very life!
atoṣyamāṇena tta devī anekaupradavo (p.c., anekaipradavo a.c.) bhavet |
tasmāt samayasatvena toṣyatīyaṃ prayatanataḥ |
aniścite pi me satvā kusīdālatthika tathā ||
sidhyate vai mukhāyānte denti māsāñ ca devatī |
aṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe tu pūjanīya prayatnataḥ |
pītapuṣpāṃ guguludhūpaṃ satkārapūjitaṃ <|> [3]
raktapīte<| ā |>nduvarṇṇābhaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaṃ |
jaṭāmakuṭadharan drīptaṃ trinetro vadanottamam |
saumyakruddhamukhaṃ bhinnaṃ pūjanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
pītapuṣpāṃ guguludhūpaṃ satkārapūjitaṃ |
divyasiddhikaraṃ pūjya anayā nānyapūjitā ||
gomāyubiḍālāsvānaś ca madhumadyāpūjitā |
kāṃsukā  raktamedāś ca baliṃ tadyāstu devatiṃ

vidyujvālākarālīsvedāṃbujaṃ mahārākṣasīsaparavidhānaṃ pravakṣyāmi ||
atha bhagavān Śākyamunis tathāgata Vijayaprabhāṣaṃ | samāsvāsya || mahāvīraṃ nāma samādhi samāpanno bhūḥ samananta[5]raḥ mahārasmaḥ ūrṇṇākodvaśād vinirgataḥ triḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtyaḥ bhagavataḥ mukhaṃ praviṣṭaḥ samanantaraḥ praviṣṭaś ca bhagavataḥ lalāṭād udakaṃ yathā muktāphalaṃ prasaviṃtam ārabdhaḥ
atha bhagavāṃ dakṣiṇapāṇi prasārya<ḥ> lalāṭāṃ parimārjya dharaṇitalaṃ tāḍitaṃ | saṃmanantaratāḍita idaṃ dharaṇitalaṃ ṣaḍvikāra kaṃpitum ārabdhaḥ || [11\verso] imāṃ dhariṇi tvā vidyujvālā nāma maṇḍala ṣaṭsu (?) bhūtaṃ |
atha maṇḍalavidhi pravakṣyāmi ||
śuklaṣṭamyāṃ śuklacatudasyāṃ vā |
yamracchati tantra vā ya<| ntra |> tu pravemañ (?) caturṣu uduṃbarakīlaṃ aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇaṃ mantreṇa saptābhimaṃṁtritaṃ kṛtvā caturṣu koneṣu hatvavyaṃ ||
bhūmiṃ khatvā jaṃghamātraṃ aṃgāratuṣāravalmīkānsaratuṣabālaasthi [2] apanīyate |
vimuktamuktas tu saugandhādaka paṃ<ca>gavyamisreṇa prakṣipet |
prakṣipya bhūmau dakacyaṃ (?) sulakṣaṇabhūmau samāsato nennatuḥ (?) supratiṣṭitāḥ paścāt sūtrayet mantradharaḥ kanyākarttitasūtraṃ pañcagavyena prakṣālya śuṣkāṃ punar api gandhodakena prakṣālayet | śuṣkāni sūtrāṇi saptābhimantritaṃ valaidvalanahasta susīkṣi[3]taṃ pūrve caturahastaṃ vā saptahastaṃ vā dvādaśahastaṃ viṃsatihastaṃ vā ekam api parikalpayet |
sucayaḥ surūpa kulodbhavaḥ dharmaparāyaṇajño sai vimuktaḥ phaṭikendratulyaṃ tatra siṃṣya ṣyai sūtra dhāraye caturdvārāṇi cihnayet vrajahūṃkāramudrābandhahastena caturdiśaṃ sūtrayet |
caturṣu koṇeṣu pūrṇṇakuṃbhapañcamaṃ madhyamaṇḍale ā[4]harttavyaṃ | apagatakeśavastreṇa baddhagrīvaṃ akālamūlakalasa saptaratnasamaṃnvitaṃ ekaikaṃ nyaset kulisaṃ kuṃbhaḥ ||
madhyamaṇḍale bhagavāṃ Śākyamunis tathāgataḥ mahāvīrasamādhiḥ ūrṇṇākoṣā vinirgatarasmayo parikalpayet || vicitrapaṭo vā yadi avidyamāne cakrapadmasaṃsthitaṃ nyaset |
tasya dakṣiṇarpārsve [5] Vajrapāṇi caturbhujāḥ dakṣiṇyaparihaste vajraṃ dvitīye kanapaḥ vāmopari hasta aṃkuśaḥ dvitīyahaste bhayamudrā samudravarṇṇāḥ savālaṃkārabhūṣitaḥ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍita raktayajñopavītaḥ || tathāgatādarśanatavadanaḥ aṣleṣakai varṇṇakaiḥ citrāpitavyaḥ yadi avidyamāne dvāviṃṣati aṃgulaṃ vajrapadmaṃ sthitaṃ [12\recto] nyaset ||
% crude gloss in left string space: vidyujvālākarālīyā dhyāna • maṃḍalakathā
tathāgatasya vāmapāsve Amoghapāśa caturbhujā | dakṣiṇoparihaste pāśa dvitīyahaste vajraparaśu vāmoparihaste vajramudgara dvitīyahaste musuṇḍī | sarvālaṃkāravibhūṣitaṃ svedāvadātasvetavastrayajñopavītaṃ jaṭāmakuṭasthitaḥ Amitābhaṃ aśle<| ke |>ṣakai varṇṇakaiś citrāpayitavyaḥ yadi avidyamāne [2] aṣṭadalapadmaṃ likhet | tasyopari pāsaṃ nyaset |
tasya cakrasya purataḥ Vidyujālākarālī svedāṃbujamahārākṣaśī dvādaśasiraḥ aṣṭādasabhujā nānāpraharaṇadharā dakṣiṇa uparihastena namaskāraṃ dakṣidvitīye khaḍgaṃ tṛtīyaste śaraṃ | caturthe cakraṃ pañcame muṣalaṃ | ṣaṣṭe trisūlaṃ saptame ghaṇṭaṃ | aṣṭame kapālaṃ | [3] navame lakuḍhaṃ | vāmaprathamena patāka | dvitīye vāraṇa | tṛtīye dhanu | caturthe aṃkuśa | pañcame kuṃta ṣaṣṭe kapālaṃ | saptame varadaṃ | aṣṭame pāṣa | navame paraṣudharaṃ | raktākṣi ūrdhadṛṣṭi sarvaduṣṭāvaṣṭa<| ((naṃ)) |><bdhaṃ> | kūra raudrākṣiṇī bhitvā kṣitisatamātraṃ darśayet |
paṭasya samaṃtā vajravidyujvālāmālāgraṃ darśayet || a[4]nāhate vastre aṣleṣakair varṇṇai citrāpayitavyaṃ | avidyamāne aṣṭādaśāyudhaṃ yaḥ pañcaragikacūrṇṇaina nyāsayet | ekaika āyudhe hrīḥkāraṃ nyaset |
bhagavatyā paṭṭayugala trayaṃ vā dātavyaḥ ekaikasiraś ca varṇṇāpaṭṭa dātavyaḥ tatraiva ekaikaṃ karṇṇo karṇṇālaṃkāra dātavyaḥ avidyamāne yathālābha dātavyaḥ [5] Śākyamunitathāgata paṭṭayugalatrayaṃ dātavyaḥ || paṭṭavastrayugalaṃ dvayaṃ Vajradharaḥ Padmadharas taḥyo yathālābhaṃ dātavyaṃ
maṇḍalasya pūrve<| ṇa |> vedike aṣṭapatravajrapadmavyavasthitā raktapadme <| vya |> svetavajraṃ | svetapadma raktavajraṃ | anenaiva prakāreṇa pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottaraṃ vā vividhaṃ ((nya))set | suvajraṃ ekaikavajra<| ṃ |>si<kā>[12\verso]ra((ṃ)) nyaset |
catuṣu koṇeṣu dhūpakaṭṭacchukāṃ pradīpa tathaiva catuṣu vyavasthitā |
ekaikadvāre dve dve trikulaṃ nyaset | ekaṃ trikaṃ caribaddhaṃ dātavyaṃ || ekāntam api dhanu tṛpañcasataṃ vā āhṛtimāyuṣai (?) devyā dātavyaḥ anenaiva prakāreṇa pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottare vyavasthāpayet | trisūla ekaikatrisūle yugalaṃ dātavyaṃ | kuli[2]saṃ kulise ekaikavastrayugalaṃ ta dāpayet |
pūrvādvāre agnikuṇḍaṃ caturasraṃ hastamātraṃ caturaṃgula vedikā karttavyāḥ
Buddhasyāgrataḥ śālyodanaṃ aṣṭādaśakhaṇḍasamaṃdhitaṃ niyojayet | anenaiva prakāreṇa Vajradharaḥ Padmadharasyāgrata sthātavyaḥ raktannadugdhakhaṇḍa raktapāṇaṃ puṣpaṃ raktacandana Bhavatyāgrataḥ sthāpayitavyaṃ [3] raktavajraraktanivedyaḥ setavajrasvetabaliḥ anenaiva prakāreṇa vyavasthāpayet |
vividhavicitrabali nānāprakāraḥ puṣpadhūpapraṇītaṃ maṇḍale samantena daśadiśaḥ baliṃ dātavyāḥ
ṣaṭmukhamudrāmantrasahitam āvāhayet | āgacchāgaccha pūrvadakṣiṇapattimottaraḥ śīghraṃ mama kāryaṃ kuru | iti vaktavyaṃ
nacaprakṣā[4]livastreṇa catudvāra pracchādayet | vajravajrāṃttare bhagavatyā mudrāhṛdaya nyāsayet ||
kanakacūrṇṇarūpyacūrṇṇa tathāvidyāmāvine iṣṭikasālipiṣṭako((dravaḥ)) sālipiṣṭakena bhinnabhedapañcaraṃgikai cūrṇṇamaṇḍalasya prayoktavyaṃ | maṇḍalasya varṇṇikāṃ aṣṭadise yojayet |
pūrvadvāre maṃtradharaḥ navavatraprāvṛtyaḥ svetavastra[5]yajñopavītasarvalakṣaṇakesāpagate vastrābaddhoṣṇīṣa Vajrapānimukhāni pīta<| ā |> pūrvādhivāsitasisyaganaṃ vyavalokya bodhisatva satvasamanvitaṃ | tadanatare pūrvasyān disi Virūḍhako nāma mahārājā | anekakuṃbhaṇḍānayutasatasahasrasamāgataḥ sādhu sādhu maṇḍalakārakaḥ sarīre balavīryaśrītejasthā[13\recto]nalakṣmī prakṣepsyāmi || atha dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi Dhṛtarāṣṭro nāma rājā anekagandharvakoṭīniyutasamāgataḥ sādhu sādhu maṇḍalakārakaḥ tava śarīre balavīryasthāmaśrīlakṣmī prakṣepsyāmi || paścimasyāṃ diśi Virūpākṣo nāma nāgarājāḥ anekaśataśahasranāgaparivārāgataḥ sādhu sādhu maṇḍalakārakaḥ tava śarīre balavī[2]ryatejaśrīsthāmalakṣmī prakṣepsyāmi | uttarasyān diśi Vaiśravaṇo nāma yakṣarājāḥ anekayakṣakoṭīniyutaśatasahasrasamanvāgata sādhu sādhu maṇḍalakārakaḥ tava śarīre tejabalavīryaśrīlakṣmīsthāma prakṣepsyāmi <|> sarājñoyo yakṣa Śrīdevī Svarasvatī Pṛthivīdevī devāsvaraṇāgayakṣagaruḍakinnamahogasamāgatā [3] sādhu sādhu maṇḍalakārakaḥ tava śarīre balavīryasthāmaśrītejalakṣmī sarve ekasvaranirghoṣeṇa prakṣepsyanti |
% NB: there is a śiṣyādhivāsanā, multiple śiṣyas
bodhicittasamanvitamantradharaḥ bodhicittaṃ sarvaparṣanmaṇḍalamadhye prakāśayet | <utthāyāsanāḥ vidyādharaḥ śiṣyagaṇaḥ pratītyasamutpādaṃ ghoṣāpayet | sapari<< + + >>gaṇaṃ gatvā dakṣiṇadvāraṃ puṣpadhūpagaṃdha<< + + >>lasya datvā paṃcāṃge namaskṛtvā punar api udghoṣayet | anenaiva prakāreṇa paścima uttare vā tri pradakṣiṇīkṛtyaḥ pūvadvāre samūhayet> tanmaṇḍalakārakaṃ prathamaṃ pracchāditamukhaṃ pravesayet | praviṣṭamātreṇa trayasthāne vajraprahāreṇa hattavyaṃ ||
paścāt puṣpāṃjaliṃ pūra[4]yet tanmantrādhiṣṭhitaṃ puṣpaṃ ghantāpayet (?) | trayo kulā asamasamaṃ kulan nirīkṣya mantramudrā dātavyāḥ taṃ puruṣaṃ gṛhītvā paścimadvāre vijakumbhasamukha | mukha nisīdayet | pariṣeṣaṃ praveśayet | kecit trisaraṇya tu gṛhītaḥ kecit samayamantramudrā parigṛhītaḥ kecit tantrānugṛhītaṃ
% NB: archaic three-kula system
% NB: there seems to be a pradhānaśiṣya, the others have options!
tanmaṇḍalapraviṣṭaṃ naveda (?) pasyaṃtu amṛ[5]tapurasya m-ālayaṃ yo kulā asamasamaṃ kulaṃ nṛpatvaṃ tu amṛtapurasya dvāraṃ pasyaṃtu amṛtapurasya bhājanaṃ gacchantu sarve bhagavaṃtaṃ kurvaṃtu buddhatvaṃ avittiyo tiṣṭaṃtu kalpakoṭīniyutasatāni ca | <| x |> yāni mayā trīṇi kalpāsaṃkhyeyā labdham anuttaraṃ padaṃ tanmaṇḍalakāraka ekajarmeṇānuttarāṃ samyaksaṃbuddhatvaṃ padaṃ prati[13\verso]lābhī bhaviṣyati
% NB: in this very life instead of three Aeons!
sarve devanāgayakṣarākṣasacāturmahārājakāyikavijayaprabhāprabhṛta<| ta |>yaḥ prakrānta iti
tanmaṇḍalasya puṣpaṃ balinivedyaṃ rajaṃ gṛhītvā nadyāṃ prakṣipet kūpe vā agninā sudagdhā pātyā vā nākramante | kākasṛgālasvānair dā na bhakṣante | tanmaṇḍalaṃ mṛdgomayasamanvita pralepayet | kim atra kṛtam i[2]ti yathā na prajñāyate ||
% NB: so that no-one will find out that an initiation has happened!

athātaḥ bhagavatyākṣepacakrabhāvanaṃ pravakṣyāmi |
dvau aṃguṣṭau vajramuṣṭi baddhvā vāmamuṣṭe tarjanīṃ prasaarya kakyasthāne nyaset | dakṣiṇamuṣṭiṃ vāmabāhe kakṣasthāne mūlākṛtī nyaset || vāmapāda dakṣiṇe[3]nākramya dakṣiṇapādaṃ vāmenākramya ūrdhvadṛṣṭimantaḥ sapta vārām ucārayet | ayam ākṣepamantraḥ thuru 2 ājjharu 2 hūṃ hūṃ | ayaṃ mahākṣepa | grāme vā nagare vā mahāgnipradīpe vā mantroccārya prayojayet takṣanod evāgni taṃbhitā bhaviṣyanti || sarvamaṇḍalasthāne yasyā rakṣaṇakāle pūrvoktaṃ gṛhyaḥ ākāśaṃ nirīkṣayet | [4] saha nirīkṣitamātreṇa ākāśa staṃbhayati | raudrakamavijayoddharaṇamahākṣepa |
dvau hastau muṣṭhiṃ badhvā utkaṭāsanasthitaḥ vāmamuṣṭhitajanīṃ prasārya dakṣiṇapādapṛṣṭhe haṃtaṃ nyaset | dakṣiṇamuṣṭhi vāmapādapṛṣṭhe tatraiva nyāsayet | ākṣepaṃ m-ākṣepamantraḥ oṃ dhuru 2 vajratuṇḍa garuvijṛṃbhita hūṃ phaṭ || saha pra[5]yojitamātreṇa ṣaṣṭiyojanāsahasraṃ bhitvā saptapātālanivāsinaḥ | asītināgasahasraṃ ca m-ucchādayati | vajragaruḍavegavijṛṃbhaṇamahākṣepaḥ
anāvṛṣṭikālasamaye vāridhārāṃ pātayet || anāvṛṣṭikālasamaye vāridhārāṃ pātayet | Vidyujvālākarālī svedāṃbujamahārākṣaśī pūrvokta[14\recto]samayamaṇḍala adhiṣṭet | pūrvasyān disi samantā Karacchatraparikaro nāma mahānāgarājā navaṣīrṣakapilagomayena sthātavyaḥ || dakṣiṇe Airāvaṇo mahānāgarājā saptaṣīrṣaḥ | paścime Naplaṃ mahānāgarājāṃ triśīrṣaḥ caturthe Sāgaro nāma nāgarājāḥ aṣṭarśīrṣaḥ [2] caturdise mahānāgapūrvoktavajragaruḍavijṛṃbhaṇamahākṣepaḥ | ekaikanāga sapta 2 vāraṃ prayojayet saha prayojitamātraḥ samaṃtā yojanasatatrayaṃ mahāvṛṣṭiṃ patāpayanti ||
atha ivākṣepaṃ dvau hastau candanena mrakṣayitvā jeṣṭhakaniṣṭha saṃkuñcya vāmamuṣṭimūlamudrā prasārya dakṣiṇe bāhusthāne nyāsayet | dakṣiṇa[3]muṣṭi vajra iva mūlamudrā prasāryaḥ vāmabāhu kasthāne nyāsayet | dau pādāṃkuri anyonya vāmapādāgraṃ ekaśūtreṇa dhārayet | hastamātraṃ prasārya jaṃghaṃ asamākṣepa mantraḥ oṃ hrī hūṃ phaṭ || hūṃ phaḥ vaisvānare hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ tiṣṭa 2 mā calaḥ yaṃ icchati taṃ vaktavyaṃ || ayaṃ ākṣepaṃ badhvā mātram ucāryaḥ raudrākṛtipreto[4]tkaṭa trikoṇamaṃḍalakathā caturmudrāmaṇḍale vā sarvamaṇḍalasthāne āhutikuṇḍe pūrvokta ākṣepaḥ sapta sapta vārāṇi yojayet | saha niyojitamātreṇa Vaisvānaraḥ sannidho bhavati yaṃ icchati taṃ siddhiṃ yācayaṃti māṇavaḥ ||
caturākṣepaṃ pravakṣyāmi hastena hastagandhā ca lipta ciṭi 2 kācimuktayukte dve muṣṭo a[5]nyonyadakṣiṇamuṣṭihṛdaye sthāne nyasya tarjanīṃ prasārya madhyamasūci anāmikāśūcīdvayapṛṣṭe jeṣṭāṃguṣṭeṇāvaṣṭebhyo jeṣṭakaniṣṭhā anyonyaṃ vāmapādapurastā pādāgrenāvakramyāḥ vāmamuṣṭi tatraiva pādajaṅgho sthāpya jeṣṭamadhyamāśūcī anyonyatarjanī prasārya sira dakṣiṇavaṃśaṃ prasārya vyak+avāmatarjanīṃ [14\verso] ekadṛṣṭibhinnabhrūstabdhanetra | dakṣiṇacakṣu dakṣiṇabāhūṃ muṣṭi mantram ucāryet || ayam ākṣepamantraḥ oṃ hūṃ oṃ huṃ payadeva hūṃ kha kha hūṃ | vighaṭitaraudrī hūṃ ā svāhā || idaṃ ākṣepamantrasahitaṃ siddhisādhanakāle bhayabhairavarūpeṇa darśayanti | vighnā pūrvoktākṣepaṃ badhvā mantram ucārya tasya saṃmukha + yojayet | saha [2] niyojitamātra nivighno bhavati | sarvasthāne nayutapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt | paṃcānaṃtaryakāriṇy api Sākyamunitathāgata Vajrapāṇimahābodhisatvaṃ Padmadharaḥ Svedāṃbujamahārākṣasy apratibhaye svapnāntare pasyati | trayaḥ kulā mānya<| ā |> dudanetrī sākyamuni caturmahākṣepaṃ
vidyotame mahāmantre vidyujvālākarālī svedāṃbu[3]jamahārākṣasī caturthaḥ <| pa |> ākṣepapalaḥ parisamāptaḥ ||   o   ||

athātaḥ bhagavanyā śātikapauṣṭikābhicārukacakrabhāvanāṃ pravakṣyāmi |

% NB: amulet-like cakras ~ yantras
prathame śāṃtikaṃ cakraṃ kathayiṣyāmi |
sahasrāracakraṃ vistayet panmasthitaṃ aṣṭāravarimadhye caturviśaṃti akṣaraṃ nyāsayet | hūṃ svāhā | hūṃ ṭha ṭha hūṃ ṭha ṭha hūṃ ṭha ṭha hūṃ ṭha ṭha hūṃ ṭha ṭha | e[4]tadāramadhye nyāsa oṃ hūṃ oṃ | cakrākṣepaṃ nyaset | prathamasanai hūṃkāraṃ tasya dakṣiṇe oṃkāra vāme oṃkāraṃ ca paścād anena akṣararājaṃ triṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya āramadhye caturviśati akṣaram | hūṃ ṭha 2 hūṃ ṭha ṭha | mama sāntir bhavati iti vaktavyaḥ anmano <| sya |> parasya vā | cakrasya upacāraḥ vastre vā phalake vā | aṣleṣakai varṇṇakai li[5]khet | cakraṃ svetacaṃdranasvetapuṣpa tato tatraiva nivedyaṃ guguludhūpaṃ dahatā triḥkālaṃ cakraṃ bhāvayem mantradharaḥ śāntike syāntikārthañ ca etallakṣaṇa bhāvayet | śāntikasya siddhilakṣaṇa upalakṣayet oṃkāre hūṃkāraṃ dṛśyate | hūṃkāre oṃkāra dṛsyate | aviparītabuddhinā amūḍhenā dṛṣṭinā pūrveṇa sthātavyāvasthitaḥ [15\recto] trayaḥ akṣararājaṃ bhāvayet || mantradharaḥ svapnāntareṇa dṛśyante subhāsubhaṃ paśyati | kṛṣṇānnapānatuṣavāla iṅgala kṛṣṇasarpagardabha uṣṭravānara kṛṣṇasvānamārjāra āvilene paśyanti | etaiḥ samastair apalakṣadvilakṣaṃ bhāvayet | tatra siddhāni svapnāntare dṛśyante | kṛṣṇānnapānavālatuṣa iṅgala etai śubhai vāmanaṃ tadduḥsvapnā[2]cchalena dṛśyante | śuklānnapānadadhikṣīradukhaghṛta bhakṣanti | svetaraktapadmaṣaṇḍaṃ dṛśyante | parvatārohanakṣīravṛkṣaphalasamanvitaṃ asvaktaṃ jarārohathocate | siddhilakṣaṇam iti | siddhamantrā iti | parasyena vaktavyaṃ | siddhisyāttare sadyam api saṃskṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvasatvarāsi parinyāgonmat anena puṇyena bu[3]ddhatvaṃ kurvaṃtu tiṣṭaṃtu kalpāni acintiyāni dharmāñ ca deśayaṃtu amṛtapadāvahāni | gṛhyaṃtu sarve te satvā satvarāsiṃ |

athātaḥ māraṇārtha soṣaṇa ucchādanāyaḥ śmasānakarmābhicāracakraṃ pravakṣya<ā><+?> guhyaṃ guhya + + + + ((kasya)) labdho[2]padeśaḥ guruparaṃparayā labdhābhiṣeka
% NB: initiation through a lineage of masters!
guhyasthāne sahasrāracakraṃ pūrveṇa raudravijṛṃbhanaṃ nāma vajraṃ nyaset | dakṣiṇe trikoṇakavidhvansanavikramamahāvajra nyaset | paścime vidyottamatejamahāvajraṃ nyase<t |> uttare merukuṃbhaṃ nāma vajraṃ nyaset | kuliśaṃ kulisasyāttare hrīḥkāraṃ hrīkāraḥ hrīkāraḥ hrīkāraḥ hrī[3]raḥ hrīkāraṃ nyaset || aṣṭāraṃ vārimadhye oṃkāraṃ kapāladvayaṃ oṃkārakapāladvayaṃ oṃkāravajraṃ nyaset <|> kapāladvayaṃ oṃkārakapāladvayaṃ āramadhye nyaset | madhyamāsūcī vāmapārsve phakāraṃ tatrai dakṣiṇe phaṭaṅkāraṃ phaṭaṅkāraṃ phaṭaṅkāraṃ nyaset | cakrākṣa oṃkāraṃ hrīkāraṃ nyaset | cakrasyāsamaṃtā jvālāmālaṃ vini[4]rgatāḥ āramadhye rudhirapūrṇṇakapālaṃ samantā aṣṭakapālaṃ nyaset |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ vā ahorātroṣito bhūtvā citrakaraiḥ mantradharaiḥ aṣlesakai varṇṇakaiḥ kapālabhājanasthitaiḥ navabālakuṃrccarudhiraḥplutaṃ kṛṣṇarakṣavarṇe dūrataḥ pracchāditavastre vā mūlakāṣṭaphalake vā likhyet | cakre rudhirabalinivedyaṃ [5] datvā pūrve raudravijṛṃbhaṇaṃ nāma mahāvajraṃ | dakṣiṇe trailokyavidhvansanavikramamahāvajraṃ tatraiva caturvajraṃ vrajrārmiḥ (?) vajraḥdṛṣṭiḥ vajravāyuḥ vajrāsaniḥ vajrolkaḥ vajravidyuḥ vajrabāluṃ | vajrameghaṃ | desarāṣṭrajanapadanimagaragrāmajanapadaiḥ etaiḥ samastaiḥ mantradhare<ṇa> krudharaudracittena bhavitavyaṃ cakraṃ <| | |> pūrvokta[16\recto]vajramayī pātayantī kimiti parijñāta
śūyārake mahānagare vare merūttarapṛṣṭe jvālāvatinātidūre Suvarṇṇabhujendro nāma rājā | Vidyujvālākarālī svedāṃbujamahārākṣaśī<ḥ> || atha sa rājalokottaravidyāvādiniḥ abhicāracakra kruddhena bhāvitaḥ ekāntanāmaṃ na gṛhṇīta dagdhā jaṃbūdvīpaṃ vajravijṛṃbhaṇabhayabhaira[2]vāḥ trailokyavidhvansanabhayabhairavāḥ vidyottamatejabhayabhairavāḥ merukumbhabhayabhairavāḥ kevalaṃ jaṃbūdvīpakruddhāḥ vādinaṃ | caturmahārājakāyikā devāv api trāyastriṃsadbhavanakurudvīpavāsina Suvarṇṇabhujendro nāmavādinaḥ gṛhaṃ sarve svabhavanavāsinaḥ āgataḥ kiṃ kruddhāvādinaḥ kathayanti devāvā[3]dine rātradhāraḥ ye svāgata bhagavanavāsinaḥ kīdṛsaṃ te bhavāntare devānuvācaḥ sarvāsanī vajramayī bhavanti devadevanikāyavinaṣṭā vajracakrabhayabhairaveṇa || takṣanāvādina vajracakrā upasaṃhāryaḥ maitrī bhāvita sarvadevatā sukhasamparita iti khagapathenātikrānta pūrvābhicāracakre ||
% NB: precedent story?
vajravijṛṃ[4]bhanaṃ nāma mahāvajravidhi upacāraḥ pūrvoktavarṇṇaraṃgai m-mālikhya raktacandanaraktakanavīrapuṣpāṃ guguludhūpaṃ dahatā vajrasya sūlākhya aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇa antare trikoṇārmikuṇḍa | kuṇḍasyāṣṭāṃgulāntara dhṛtarāṣṭramahārājā ekamanakṛtāṃjalayaḥ muktābharaṇāmuktasikhaiḥ citrāpayet raktakara[5]vīrapuṣpāṃ raktacandanaguguludhūpaṃ dahatā dvātriṃsati akṣaraṃ āradhye kulisāntare <| cū |> sūcīmantra phaṭkāraṃ cakrākṣe | oṃ hrīr iti krūra pūrvokta<| ā |>mūlamudrāṃ baddhvā vāmadakṣiṇyacakraṃ bhāvya pūrve raudravijṛṃbhaṇamahāvajre triḥkāraṃ nyaset | kumāṇḍapṛṣṭe vajraṃkusaṃ nyaset || dakṣiṇe trilok+avidhvaṃsanamahāva[16\verso]vajra madhyamāsūcī agre <| dha |> aṣṭāṅgulātta trikoṇārmikuṇḍaṃ tasyāṣṭāṅgulānte virūḍhakaṃ mahārājāna muktābharaṇa kṛtakarapuṭaiḥ muktasikhen tasya pṛṣṭe vidyotamamahāvajraṃ madhyamasūci agre aṣṭāṃgul<| i |>atve trikoṇyagnikuṃḍaṃ tasyāṣṭāṅgulaś ca virūpākṣo mahārājā muktābharaṇakṛtakarapuṭaḥ muktasikhaḥ tasya sireṇa vā pāṇiratneta manicūḍaṃ tasya pṛṣṭe bhrūṃkāra nyaset | uttare merukumbha nāma mahāvajraṃ pūrvoktavarṇṇena citrāpayet || madhyamā sūc<| i |>ī ākāraṃ tasyāgre aṣṭāṃgulāṃte trikoṇam agnikuṇḍaṃ tasyāṣṭāṅgulāṃte vaiśravaṇaṃ mahārā[4]jānāṃ || vāmajaṃ pṛthivyāṃ pratiṣṭāpyaḥ muktābharaṇa kṛtakarapuṭāḥ tasya pṛṣṭena vajrāṃtena mudgaraṃ caturmahārājakāyikāpṛṣṭe aṣṭāṅgupramāṇaṃ vedikā uttarapūrvakoṇe samasūtraṃ pūrvadakṣiṇe tathā | dakṣiṇe sarīrabhasmaprapalāpitasvapitakaṭṭārīhasta anenaiva prakāreṇa pūrvokte kṛṣṇaraktabhūmivarṇena caturdise citrāpayet | pūrvavedikāsamantatrayosthāne dārusaṃcayamṛtaśavaḥ saṃskārākṛtiṃ dhūmāgniṃ darśayet natrāntarai [17\recto] śṛgālair darśayet | anenaiva prakāreṇa pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottaraṃ mahāsmasānaṃ darśayet pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottaraṃ mahāsmasānaṃ darśayet | pūrvadevīmadhye <ba>li pūrvoktamudrāmantrasahitaṃ dhṛtarāṣṭrasyāgrataḥ citrapayi[3]tavyaḥ madyapūrṇaṃ mansyamānsasahitaṃ virūḍhakasyāgrataḥ citrāpayitavyaḥ lājasarṣapamadhusalilamisraṃ virūpākṣasyāgrataḥ citrāpayet caturṣe jambuḍimisra kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ dadimisraṃ kuverasyāgrataḥ citrāpayet || etai<ḥ> samastaiḥ ṣaṣṭisya vasthitaiḥ || anenaiva vidhinā pūrvoktaṃ vidhinā citrāpayitavyaḥ sama[4]ntā nānāvarṇaraktakṛṣṇapuṣpaiḥ raktacandana āla<|¯|>ktakarasaṃ sarṣapamisra ākṛtima mansyamānsasarudhiraḥ yadi avidyamāne kṛtimambā guguludhūpaṃ dahatā cakrapaṭasya sāmantake datvā bhagavanyā mūlamurdramantrasahitaṃ badhvā caturṣu vajra prayojaye punar api caturṣu ākṣepaṃ badhvā mantrasahitaṃ caturṣu vajre prayo[5]jayet || punar api pūrvoktagandhapuṣpabaṃliṃ datvā upasaṃhāryaḥ gāḍhena saṃceṭalākṣeṇa mudrāpayet |
citrakarai nar darśayitavyaḥ nirākule asaṃkīrṇasucisthāne parvatasikhare parvatagahvare śūnyadevāyatane śūnyagrahe vā svagṛhabhūmihare vā | ekatra sthāne gṛhyaḥ caturhasta caturdvāraṃ sa vicakṣaṇa baddhakuṇḍa[17\verso]mṛtiṃ vā vaniliptasuyaṃtraghaṭitaiḥ sabdena sabdā niḥkramati gṛhaṃ bhūmyā caturaṃgula sodhayitvā punar api samāsa bhūmyā hattavyaṃ | sulakṣaṇe ruddhasva<ra> suhitai dvārabandhaiḥ kīlakīlanadvārayatraiḥ upasobhitagṛhasamattaiḥ caturṣu khadirakīlaṃ soḍasāṃgulapramāṇaṃ ekāgrāya samayī ekīkakīle hrīḥkāra nyasye pūrva uttara[2]koṇe gṛhya dakṣiṇāmukhamantradharaiḥ pūrvoktakīlamudrā mantrasahitaṃ badhvā sapta vārāṃ kīlasya niyojayitvā śīghraṃ hattavyaḥ gandhā pūrvadakṣiṇakoṇaṃ punar api kīlamudrāṃ badhvā sapta vāra kīla prayojyaḥ pūrvadakṣiṇakoṇe śrīghraṃ hattavyaḥ || anenaiva prakāreṇa dakṣiṇapaścimakoṇe hattavyaḥ paścimottarakoṇe tathā prayokta[3]vyaḥ gṛha sāmaṃtaka kṛṣṇarakta nānāpuṣpaiḥ guguludhūpaṃ dahatā caturṣu koṇe kīlaṃ rudhiramisraṃ salilasarṣapānvitāṃ | lākṣāraseṇa kīlaṃ snāp<| i |>ayet caturṣu kīla hasta druṣṇa upāsāmya snāpayet | divasadivasāṃtaraṃ dharmi prayojayet |
% NB here the practitioner is a mantradhara
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇacaturdasyāṃ ahorātroṣito bhūtvā gṛhaṃ pravisya vidyujvālākarālī svedāṃbu[4]jamahārākṣasī caturṣu koneṣu sapta vārāṃ prayojya brahmasthāne ubhayataḥ dakṣiṇāmukhaṃ mūlamudrā mantrasahitaṃ badhvā vidyujvālākarālī svedāṃbujamahārākṣasī dvādvasasiraṃ aṣṭādasabhujā cintayet | paścāc cakrapaṭaṃ uttarābhimukha sthāpayet | pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpadīpanivedya pūrvoktavidhinā paṭasya datvā gṛhaṃ ni[5]nikrāmya disābandhaṃ mantramudrāsahitaṃ śīmābandhaṃ prākāra<| ja |>pañjarakīlaṃ dharaṇibandhaṃ samaṃtā daśadisaṃ pūrvoktamantramudrāsahitaṃ sve sve dvayaḥ saṃyojyaḥ amūḍhe<va> gandhamudrāmantrasahitaṃ puṣpaṃ puṣpamudrā tathā nivedya pradīpaṃ dhūpaṃ matsyamānsarudhirabali gṛhasamaṃtā daśadisaṃ datvā punar api hastapādā prakṣālya gṛhaṃ pravi[18\recto]sya paṭa<| la |>syābhimukhaṃ raudravikramaḥ vijayo dhā<da>(?)raṇa mahākṣepaṃ badhvā mantram ucārayet || paṭasya pūrva dhṛtarāṣṭro mahārājā saptadhā prayojayet | vidyuvikrāntamahākṣepaṃ badhvā mantrasahitaṃ cakrasya dakṣiṇadise virūḍhakā nāma mahārājā <| <vijṛ>mbhana mahāsamudramantram ucārya cakrasya |> sapta vārāṃ niyojayet | vajragaru[2]ḍavijṛṃbhaṇamahākṣepaṃ badhvā mantram ucāryaḥ | cakrasya paścime virūpākṣo nāma mahārājā sapta vārāṃ prayojayet tayastriṃsavigalitapriyadevamahākṣepaṃ badhvā mantra ucārya || uttare kuverasya sapta vārāṃ niyojayet | etaiḥ catuṣu mahārājaiḥ saṃmukhā svetavijṛṃbhaṇamahāmudrāmantra ucārya cakrasya pū[3]rve dhṛtarāṣṭramahārājā pṛṣṭhe sapta vārāṇiṃ yojayet | paścād buddhanetreṇa kīlayāmi mama kāryaṃ kuru r-iti cittayet || vidyuvajrī mahāmudrāṃ badhvāṃ mantram ucārya dakṣiṇe virūḍhako mahārāja pṛṣṭhe sapta vārāṃ niyojayet | buddhavijayavidyu mama kīlayāmi mama kāryaṃ kuru r-iti citayet || mahāvijṛṃbhaṇamahā[4]mudrāṃ badhvā mantram ucāryaḥ paścime virūpākṣamahārājā pṛṣṭhe sapta vāraṃ niyojya mama kāryaṃ kuru r-iti cintayet || ṣaṭmukhamahāmudrāṃ badhvā mantram ucāryaḥ kuverasya pṛṣṭhe sapta vāran niyojayet | mama kāryaṃ kuru r-iti citayet |
paṭasyāgrataḥ caturṣu kuṇḍa trikoṇaṃ sulakṣaṇa samasūtra pūrve prathamakuṇḍa paṭa[5]syāṃtare vajrāṃkusa mṛnmayī vāma padmasthitaṃ || dvitīyakuṇḍa paṭalasyāntare vajraparśu kaṣṭhayī mṛmayī vā padmasthitaṃ || tṛtīyakuṇḍa <paṭa>syāntare pārsve tṛṇarājamayī raktavastramayī vā padmitaṃ || caturthakuṇḍa paṭasyāntare khadiramayaṃ vajramudganaṃ padmasthitāṃ etaiḥ samastaiḥ alaktakaraṃjitaiḥ
smasānakāṣṭhair aṣṭhāṃgu[18\verso]lapramāṇaṃ krodhavajraṃ pañcasūcī rājikāmisraṃ kapālasthitaṃ || pūrvakuṇḍe sādhakasyāntare sthātavyaṃ || smasānakāṣṭamayī vajraṃ ṣaḍaṅgulapramāṇaṃ pañcasūcī vinirgatarasmayaḥ rājikāmisraṃ kapālasthitaṃ || dvitīyakuṇḍa sādhakasyāttare sthātavyaṃ smasānakāṣṭā nimbamayī vā aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇaṃ krodhavajraṃ pañcasūcikaṃ [2] rājikāmisraṃ kapālasthitaṃ || tṛtīyakuṇḍe sādhakasyāntare sthātavyaṃ | smasānakhadirakāṣṭhamayī ṣaḍāṃgulapramāṇaṃ krodhakajraṃ pañcasūcī vinirgatarasmayaḥ rājikasahitaṃ kapālasthita etaiḥ samastaiḥ kulisai alaktakarasena prokṣayitavyaṃ ||
pūrvaprathamakuṃḍe raudravikramavijayoddhāraṇamahākṣepaṃ badhvā maṃtram ucā[3]rya pūrvaprathamakuṇḍe vajraṃ vajrāṃkuse niyojayet | kruddhadṛṣṭi nirīkṣayet | paṭasya dhṛtarāṣṭravidyuvikrāṃtamahākṣepaṃ badhvā mantram ucāryaḥ pūrvadvitīye<| ya |> kuṇḍe vajraṃ vajraparaśu niyojayet | <paṭasya dakṣiṇe virūḍhako mahārājā kruddhadṛṣṭi niyojayet> vajragaruḍavijṛṃbhanamahākṣepaṃ badhvā maṃtram ucāryaḥ tṛtīyakuṇḍe vajraṃ vajra<| ¯ |>ṃ<| ku |>pāsaṃ niyojayet || paṭasya paścime virūpākṣamahārā[4]jā kruddhaṃdṛṣṭi trayastriṃsavigalitapriyadevamahākṣepaṃ badhvā mantram ucārya varṇṇakuṇṭhavajravajramudgaraṃ niyojyaḥ paṭasya uttare kuberamahārājāḥ kruddhadṛṣṭi mantradharaḥ pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpadīpamālyopahāraṃ datvā bhagavatyā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā paṭasya caturdise mahāvajrakrodhaṃ niyojayet | āsanamudrāṃ badhvā āsane niyojayet [5] vajrapadmagarbha niṣpādya bandhasthitaḥ padmāsane cakraṃ vāmena bhavyaṃ avasāna meruktaṃ bhavāmasūcī prathamaṃ tatraiva dakṣiṇe sūcī anenaiva prakāreṇa gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ nivedyaṃ pradīpaṃ pūrvoktamantramudrāsahitaṃ paścā dine dine cakraṃ ekalakṣaṃ japet dvilakṣeṇe ardhalakṣe viparītatāyā yānti akṣararājā yaṭmukhamudrāmaṃtrasahitaṃ ba[19\recto]dhvā kruddha caturmahārājāṃta japet | akṣararājaṃ nirīkṣyaḥ punar api tāḍayet | akṣarasaṃsthāne vyavasthitaḥ upa<la>kṣya pūrvoktavidhinā cakraṃ bhāvayet || lakṣyābhyantara trayakālaṃ vighnaṃ darśayati | kule ekaikaṃ dattaṃ asokāṃ cūṭa viṃvṛkṣa bhakṣaś cūṃcatur vighnā cāturmahārājakāyikā bhagavanapālakāpālakaḥ anenaiva [2] prakāreṇa ekalakṣaṃ pūrayet vidyādharaḥ samantā gandhaṃ puṣpadhūpaṃ bali rudhiraṃ matsyamānsaṃ daśadisaṃ datvā kulisaṃ ekaikaṃ kulise pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpabaliḥ pradīpaṃ dviguṇaṃ dadyāt ekaikakulise mahātaṃ pūjā satkāraṃ siddhisiddhapaṭasya tathā upasaṃhārya pūjāsatkārya mṛdgomayasamanvitaṃ upalipya gṛhaṃ [3] tatrokta gandhapuṣpadhūpabalirudhiraṃmatsyamāṃsa daśadisaṃ paṭasya samantā datvā dine dine cakraṃ bhāvayed yāvat kṛpakṣaṃ dināni āsanna divase kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇacaturdasyāṃ vā ahorātroṣito bhūtvā mantradharaḥ ṣaṭmukhaṃ mantrasahitaṃ badhvā paṭādhivāsya ekarātraṃ cintayet | pūrvaprathamakuṇḍe smasānakāṣṭer agni prajvālya cakra sapta vārā bhāva[4]yitvā rājikāhutī sapta vārāṃ satrunāma gṛhītavyaṃ gṛhītamātreṇa indrāsaniḥ patati vigatas tathā
% NB practitioner here vidyādhara or mantradhara
lavaṇāsūcī viṣaphālgusamativarṇṇāḥ etaiḥ samastaiḥ ekīkṛtya satrupratimaṃ kṛtvā cakrapaṭasya ekavārāṃ bhāvayet | tatprakṛtikṛtiṃ kṛtvā ekatra vajraṃ gṛhya tatpratikṛtināmaṃ gṛhya hattavyaḥ saha hanitamātreṇa rāja[5]satruḥ puranimisūḥ unmīlayanti | vajrāgnim iva patitas tathā |
pratyānayana karttam icchā sādhakena pūrve hataṃ vajra gṛhya kṣīrasarṣapamisreṇa snāpayet | bhagavanyā mūlamudrāmantrasahitaṃ sapta vārāṃ mantramudrām uccārya amukaṃ sāṃti bhavanv iti vaktavyaḥ
pūrvokta caturṣu kuṇḍaṃ prathamam aṃkuśakuṇḍaṃ | dvitīyaṃ vajraparaśukuṇḍaṃ tathā tṛ[19\verso]tīyaṃ vajrapāśakuṇḍaṃ caturthaṃ vajramudgaraṃs tathā | caturmahārājakāyikā devāḥ caturṣu kuṇḍabheda ninnapā datvāhatiṃ cittacaitasika na cāgni na ca samidhāḥ na midbhiḥ nādyaṃ na ca tailatṛmadhuraṃ na cāṣekṣaṃ na ca tṛśukla kevalaṃ cakrapaṭaṃ bhāvayet ante rājikā gṛhya paṭasyapūrve dhṛtarāṣṭrasyāgrataḥ kuṇḍa sapta vārāṃ bhāvayet [2] bhāvayitvāhutiṃ kuryād amukaṃ māraye<| t |>ti pratyāyanaṃ nāsti | sūparīkṣitavyaṃ |
% NB no reversal, killing is final
kṛṣṇatilaṃ gṛhya viseṣaṇa bhāvayet | śuṣkaṃ cakrapaṭasya dakṣiṇe virūḍhakasyāgrataḥ kuṇḍe sapta vārāṃ cakrasya bhāvyaḥ pūrvokta tila<| dhu |> gṛhya juyāt | satruṇāmaṃ gṛhītamātre saptasatayojanābhyantare śatrava | kariṣyati punar api sānti[3]r bhavati ||
sarṣapasmasānabhasmaṃ ekīkṛtya kṛtya cūrṇṇayitvā rudhiraplutaiḥ gulikā kārayet kālāvamātraiḥ tadgulikāṃ gṛhya paṭasya paścime virūpākṣo mahārājāgrataḥ kuṇḍe cakrapaṭaṃ sapta vārāṃ bhāvayed āhutiṃ kuryāt satrunāmaṃ gṛhya <gṛ>hītamātraḥ satravo di<g>paryaṃte satravaḥ kariṣyanti | punar api pratyānayanaṃ bhavati || su[4]sukhabījaṃ rudhireṇa bhāvayet | śuṣkān tadbījaṃ gṛhyaḥ uttare kuberasyāgrataḥ kuṇḍe cakrasya sapta bhāvyāhuti kuryāt satrunāmaṃ gṛhītavyaṃ itarajanai na kadācit kuryāl
% NB: use of cremation ground ashes
lokottaravādinaḥ stūpasthāne liṃgavyavasthāpinaḥ pratibiṃbagṛhyasthāne anyadevagṛhapratiṣṭhāpayitumanasya vādinaḥ vihārasmai īrṣyālaukikavādinaḥ lo[5]ttaravāditaṃ da nirīkṣya pūrvoktapaṭe caturṣu bhāvanā kuṇḍe sve sve dravyaṃ yojyā ādivasāne ṣaṭmukhaṃ caturmahārājā prayojya lokottaravidyādhareṇa laukikavādina mārayitavyaḥ
% NB: the superworldly vidyādhara should kill those who establish liṅgas where stūpas stood, consecrate other deities where [Buddhist] images were, and speak with envy against monasteries, implying that they are worldly (?)
athātaḥ caturṣu agnikuṇḍe māraṇaṃ || kṣatriyaṃ kṣatriyapramadāṃ rājā mantriḥ tatpramadā vā | brahmaṇaḥ brāhmaṇapramadā vā | vaiśyaḥ vaiśyapramadā vā | vṛṣala<ḥ> [20\recto] vṛṣalī vā | anyatamaḥ anyatamo vā duṣṭa bahuvidhā | tarjānaṃ tāḍanaṃ māraṇaṃ uccāṭanaṃ soṣaṇaṃ cchedanaṃ bhedanaṃ kīlaṇaṃ dveṣaṇaṃ ucchvāsanaṃ ārocakaṃ raktaśrāvanaṃ indiyabandhaṃ vicchinnaṃ gamaṇaṃ anyavikramanaṃ dvārabandhaṃ etaiḥ samastaiḥ raudraraudrakarma niyojayet |
prathamaṃ kṣatriyadayumāraṇaṃ kuṇaṭim gugulu mānusāsthicūrṇṇaṃ | viṣaṃ [2] samudrodbhavaṃ etaiḥ samabhāgaiḥ nṛpatipratikṛtiṃ sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitaṃ tasya hṛdaye nāmam ālikhya pūrvoktavajraṃkuśakuṇḍe kāñcanakāṣṭair agni prajvālya tatpratikṛtiṃ sthitvā cakrapaṭa ekabhāvyaṃ juhuyāt | vajrapāṇinā śatrunāmaṃ gṛhītavyaḥ saha gṛhītramātraḥ rājā sāṃtaḥpura rājā saptasthitaḥ mṛta kriyaṃte || pratyānayana yadi neccha[3]ti sādhakaḥ tadā āhutibhasmaṃ gṛhya gugulumisrāṃ guḍikāṃ kuryāt | punar api kāñcanakāṣṭair agni prajvālya sapta guḍikāṃ gṛhya paṭa sapta vārāṃ bhāvya ekaikaguḍike tasya nāmam ālikhya juhuyāt | vajrapāninā śatruṇāmaṃ gṛhītavyaḥ sa gṛhītavyamātraṃ pramadā sapta mṛṃta gṛhītavyaḥ sa gṛhītamātraṃ pramadā sapta mata iti sabdaṃ śruyaṃ[4]te pratyānayanaṃ karttavyaḥ cakrapaṭasya mahatīṃ pūjāsatkāram ārabhya caturṣu vajraṃ kṣīreṇa snāpayet | caturmahārājā pūrvoktagandhandhapuṣpadhūpamālyopahāraṃ goṣīrṣacandanaiḥ rājā rājapramadā vā pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā svet puṣpaṃ sugandhadhūpa bhagavanyā datvā pūrvoktamūlamudrāṃ badhvā sapta vārāṃ tatpratikṛti niyojayet || catuṣu vajraṃ tathā cakrapaṭaṃ [5] dakṣiṇena bhāvya sapta vārāṃ tatpratikṛtiṃ sāntir bhavatu iti o mārjayet śāntir bhavati | tadguḍi pūrvoktalikā ekāṃ gṛhya saptabhāgaṃ kṛtvā pūrvoktakāṃcaṇakāṣṭair agniṃ prajvālya cakrapaṭavāme sapta bhāvyaḥ satrunāmaṃ gṛhya juhuyāt saha nāmagṛhītamātra mariṣyanti pratyānayanaṃ karttavyaḥ taṃḍulata(?)madhurasarṣapamisraṃ juhuyāt sānti[20\verso]r bhavati |
% NB magic against kings
tad bhasmaṃ gṛhya asthicūrṇṇaṃ bhujāṃgagandhacaṃviṣaṃ nakulacarmaṃ mārjāradāḍhaṃ cchuchundirikā sakalāni mūṣakai tathā siramayūragogardabhatudasthituragalāṅgalīkhajarīṭahastinī ulūkākākapakṣaiḥ etaiḥ samabhāgaiḥ pūrvoktabhasmasahitaṃ smasānaghaṭike smasānakāṣṭair agniṃ prajvālya pacet (?) taila(?)sṛdāgninā samāptapaṭānta ghṛ[2]ṣṭe kapālacaturthe kapāle pūrayitvā caturṣu kuṇḍe smasānakāṣṭair agni prajvālya mahānīlam ābhyaṃgaṃ vyāghracarmagajacarmarājabalācarma etaiḥ samabhāgair ekīkṛtya ekaikakuṇḍe sapta 2 vārāṃ juhuyāt | caturṣu kuṇḍe bhasmaṃ gṛhya iṅgalavimuktaiḥ sūtthaiḥ pūrvokta caturṣu kapālasthitaḥ tailena tad bhasmaṃ prakṣipya ekaikapāla sairṇṇapatreṇa [3] pracchādayet || paṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpayitavyaḥ saptataḥ samānte saptame divase tailaṃ gṛhya ekīkṛtya āyasamayībhājane prakṣipya karpūrakakkola etaiḥ tṛkakuṣṭair etāni samabhāgāni śuṣkaṃ cūrṇṇa kṛtvā tattailamadhye prakṣipya supihitaṃ kārayet | ripudāruṇaṃ vidveṣatailaṃ trilokavikhyātaṃ vajravad bhidyate dehaḥ
mānuṣāsthi[4]mayaṃ pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā sirāṃ brahmasthāne ekāṃgulasiraṃ pūrvoktatailaṃ ekaikabindususire prakṣipya pūrvoktamayiṇavastreṇa suvihitaṃ cakra ekabhāvitaṃ nāmaṃ gṛhya smasāne nikhattavyaḥ saha nikhanitamātraḥ mahāvidveśa karoti | uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ
<sittha>kamayī pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā tasya nāmaṃ hṛdaye m-ālikhya pūrvoktatailaṃ gṛhya tatpratikṛtiṃ kṛ[5]tvā makṣrayitvā khadirakāṣṭair agniṃ prajvālya tatpratikṛtiṃ tāpaye cchanai 2 paṭaikabhāvitaṃ saha tāpitamātraḥ mah<| i |>atā jārārogena avaṣṭabdhavidveṣakaro bhavati | uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ ||
tanmayīpratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā kṣīreṇa sarṣapamisraṃ sthāpayet bhagavatyā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā mantrasahitaṃ sapta vārāṃ niyojya sānti bhavatv iti | cartu[21\recto]rṣu kulisaṃ gandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ pūrvoktavidhinā datvā caturmahārājakāyikānān devā tathaiva ca mukta mocayituṃ duḥkhaṃ ||
smaśānamṛtikāṃ gṛhya <| va |> lavaṇasarṣapamisraṃ pīthayitvā yasya kasya cidīrṣya kasya pratikṛti kṛtvā indriyasthāne ūṣṇā<| ¯ |>gnivarṇṇasūcī hattavyaḥ nāmaṃ gṛhya sūcīm udhṛtya pūrvoktatailea susire prakṣipya smaśānakā[2]ṣṭair agni prajvālya tapratikṛtiṃ tāpayet | paṭa eva bhāvituṃ bhāvitaḥ saha bhāvitaḥ mātra mahatā vidveṣakārī bhavati | pratyānayanaṃ karttavyaṃ tanmayī pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā pūrvoktabhagavatyā mūlamudrā badhvā mantrasahitaṃ pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ cakrapaṭasya datvā cāturmahārajikāyikā devā tathaiva ca || caturṣu kulisakulise pūrvo[3]ktagandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ datvā paścā ṣaṭmukhamudrāṃ badhvā mantrasahitaṃ tatpratikṛtipṛṣṭe niyojayet tasya sānti bhavatv iti niyojayet saha niyojitamātraḥ mahāśāntir bhavati |
etad adṛṣṭaguhyavidveṣakaḥ sparsavidveṣo bhavati |

rājā vā rājapramadā vā  rājamantriṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā vaiṣyavṛṣala tathā | pūrvoktatailasirasi vā gā[4]tre abhyaṅga vilepayet puṣpapādatale pata tailaṃ pṛṣṭhamātra mahatā vidveṣo bhavati | pratyānayanaṃ bhavati | sarvavidveṣakāṃ kṣīraṃ sarṣpa samasānabhasmaṃ tailāṃśu ekīkṛtya bhagavatyām upahāryaṃ kuryāt | oṃduṃbarakāṣṭenāgni prajvālya kṣ<ī>rasarṣapāhutīm aṣṭaśataṃ juhuyāt | sarvaviduṣṭā muktā bhavanti |
mānuṣāsthimayaṃ kīlakaṃ [5] aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇaṃ pūrvoktatailaṃ mrakṣayitvā tasya nāmaṃ likheṃntam ālikhya cakrapaṭa<| ṃ saṣṭana(?) |><bhā>vitaṃ śatruṇāmaṃ gṛhya catuṣpathe hattavyaḥ saha hanitamātra mahāvidveso bhavati | uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ ||
khadiramayaṃ kīlakaṃ m-aṣṭāṅgulapramāṇa<| ¯ |>ṃ tasya nāmaṃ kīle m-ālikhya pūrvoktatailaṃ mrakṣayitvā + + ṃ + prabhāvya sayanasthāne nikhatavyam [21\verso] saha khanitamātraḥ sayana kaṃpitā mahatā vidvoṣo tadgṛhaṃ bhavati | uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ ||
mānuṣāsthimayaṃ kīlakaṃ caturṣu ṣaḍaṅgulapramāṇāṃ gṛhya caturṣu kīlam ālikhya pūrvoktatailaṃ mrakṣayitvā paṭaṃ sapta bhāvya gṛhya caturṣu koṇai mūrtthā hatavyaḥ saha hanitamātra gṛha samantāt brahmarākṣasa + + sa dṛsyate mahata ucchvāse gṛhā ni[2]kramya prapalāyanti | taṃ gṛhaṃ varṣasahasram api mānusā na prabhavanti | śāntir bhavati | suparīkṣiti karttavyaḥ
madanakāṣṭhenāgnim prajvālyaḥ pūrvoktas tailam aṣṭottarasatatailabiṃdu juhuyāt | tad bhasmaṃ gṛhya pūrvoktatailamisraṃ ekīkṛtya jape taḍāge vā kṣipet tataḥ (after some correction) kūpe taḍāge vā salilaṃ spṛṣṭamātre sanagaragrāma mahatā vidve[3]ṣeṇa pralayaṃ yāsyanti mānuṣāḥ pratyānayanaṃ kakturm icchati sādhakena tannagaramadhye vidyujvālākarālīsvedāṃbujamahārākṣasīsamayamaṇḍalādhiṣṭhe pūrvoktapañcaghaṭe kṣīrapūrṇṇaṃ caturṣu koṇe pañcamamadhyamaṇḍale sthātavyaḥ pūrvoktagandhapuṣpamālyāpahāram maṇḍalasya samaṃtād daśadiśaṃ datvā saptāha mahatā pūjāsatkāram ā[4]rabhya bhurbhuvasvādaya bhagavatyā jayajayaśabdo tra ghoṣya pañcaghaṭe kṣīraṃ gṛhya kūpaṃ taḍāge tat kṣīraṃ prakṣipyaḥ paścā ṣaṭmukhaṃ mudrāmantrasahitaṃ sapta vārāṃ kūpe taḍāge niyojayet paścād amṛtodakaṃ bhatīti vakatavyaṃ mahāvidveṣako mukto bhavati ||
pūrvacakrapaṭasya vajravijṛṃbhanaṃ nāma mahāvajradūragatapracchāditavastre satru[5]pratikṛtiṃ cakrapaṭasya raktavarṇṇena citrāpayet | tasya sarīre brahmasthāne pūrvoktavajrahanitapraviṣṭatrisūcīkulisakrodhaḥ trisūcīṃ darśayet madhyamāsūcīṃ hrīṃkāraṃ nyasya vāmapārsve phaṭkāraṃ dakṣiṇapārsve tathaiva ca satru gṛhṇāmīti cakrapaṭasya sapta dhyāyī tat paṭaṃ trikoṇaṃ veṣṭayitvā smasāneṣu cirasaskāritabhūmiprade[22\recto]se mūlamudrāṃ badhvā nikhanitamātraḥ rājā vā rājamaṃtrī vā mṛyate na śāntir bhavati | suparīkṣitaṃ karttavyaḥ <||>
% NB the injunction to examine the situation carefully where there is no reversing magic
pūrvoktavastre satrupratikṛtim ālikhyaḥ tasya sire trailokyavidhvansanavikramamahāvajrahatasire praviṣṭakulisāt pañca sūcīn darśayet | madhyamasūcī hrīkāra vāmapārsve phaṭkāra dakṣiṇa tathaiva tatpaṭṭa trikoṇa veṣṭayi[2]tvā cakrapaṭa sapta vyāpī satrunāmaṃ gṛhyaḥ sa<| tya |>dya ādiśya sthāne nikhantavyaḥ saha nikhanitamātraḥ rājā vā rājabhāryā vā rājamaṃtrī vā mṛyate punar api śāntir bhavati |
pūrvoktavidhinā satrupratikṛtim ālikhyaḥ paścime vidyotamamahāvajraṃ tasya sire<| ¯ |> hatākṛti trisūcī darśayet madhyamāsūcīṃ hrīṃkāraṃ vāmapārsve pha[3]ṭkāraṃ dakṣiṇapārsve tathaiva ca | tatpaṭaṃ gṛhya trikoṇayitvā vedayet || mūlamudrāṃ badhvā paṭa sapta bhāvyaṃ smasāne nikhatavyaṃ satruṇāmaṃ gṛhītavya sa gṛhītamātraḥ rājā vā rājamantrī vā sadya mṛte śāntir bhavati |
tatraiva vastravarṇṇasaṃyukte satrupratikṛtim ālikhya pralaṃbabāhuḥ muktasikhaḥ meruktaṃbhamahāvajraṃ tasya sira[4]sthāne hatākṛtiṃ paṃcasūcīṃ darśayet | madhyamāsūcīṃ hrīkāraṃ tasya vāmapārsve phaṭkāra dakṣiṇapārsve tathaiva ca tatpaṭe trikoṇayitvā veḍhayet paṭa sapta bhāvyaḥ mūlamudrāṃ badhvā satruṇāmaṃ gṛhya smasāne nikhatavyaḥ saha nikhanitamātrena sarvaduṣṭarājā vā rājamantrī vā mṛyate | śāntir bhavati | surīkṣya karttavyaḥ cāturmahārā[5]ja tasyāṃ tasyāṃ viseṣa ṣamukhamudrāmantrasahitaṃ niyojayet | saha niyojitamātraḥ sve sve karmāṇi śīghraṃ siddhiṃ kariṣyanti || o ||

% henceforth añjanas
srotāñjanaṃ paṭasyāgrataḥ kapālasthita sthāpyaḥ cakrapaṭasyāṣṭottarasataṃ bhāvyaṃ akṣiṇy añjayet | adṛsyo bhavati adṛsyena vi + +ṃ p+tiṣyad bhāgene(?)kabhāgaṃ gṛhya upabhuṃ[22\verso]jya āśā bhagavatyā dātavyā
haritālaṃ gorocanaṃ ekīkṛtya kapālasthitaḥ paṭasyāgrataḥ sthātavyaṃ cakrapaṭasya nayanaṃ bhāvyaṃ tad dravyaṃ kapilaghṛta ekīkṛtya caturṣu mahāvajre nāmaṃ gṛhya caturṣu mahārājāna tatraiva ca pūrvoktaḥ gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpamālyopahārāṃ kṛtvā cakrapaṭasya + + cchati adatvā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā punar a[2]pi cakrapaṭa nayutaṃ bhāvayet | yadi na ūṣmāyati ṣaṭmukhamudrāṃ badhvā mantrasahitaṃ caturṣu mahārājā tarjayet | punar api nasūtabhāvite ūsmāyati tad dravyaṃ gṛhya pādālepa gacchet yojanasatatrayaṃ gata punar apy āgacchati | ekadivase yojanasaptasataṃ gatvā dvitīye divase āgacchati ||
nāgakāṣṭaiḥ nāgakanyā[3]pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā cakrapaṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpya nāgakāṣṭair agni prajvālyaḥ nāgakeśaraḥ gugulumisraṃ pūrvoktavajrāṃkuśakuṇḍe aṣṭotarasata paṭaṃ bhāvyaṃ juhuyāt tatpratikṛtiṃ āhuti bhasmaṃ kañjikamisraṃ tatpratikṛtiṃ mrakṣayitvā bhagavanyā pādamūle nikhattavyaṃ saha nikhanitamātrā sarve duṣṭā nāgā uccāṭayati sarvā nāga<| ¯ |>ka[4]nyā vasyā bhavanti | yasya kasyaci duṣṭaviṣaṃ duṣṭena upaharati | vādinaḥ uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ
% NB opponent = vādinaḥ
devadārukāṣṭair devapratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā cakrapaṭasyāgrataḥ sthātavyaṃ pūrvoktavajraparasukuṇḍaṃ devadāsakāṣṭair agni prajvālyaḥ rājikāviṣamisraṃ | aṣṭotaraśata paṭa bhāvya juhuyāt | tad bhasmaṃ gṛhyaḥ kañjikenālocya pratikṛtiṃ mrakṣa[5]yitvā smasāne nikhatavyaṃ ṣaṭmukhamudrāṃ badhvā mantrasahitaṃ tasyopari raktacandanasalilamisreṇa snāpayet sadevakulaṃ pralayaṃ yānti sarvadevā uccāṭanakarmaḥ || uddhṛtya sarvadevā sukha samarpitāḥ
% NB chasing away [foreign] deities
bhagavatyā cakrapaṭaṃ mahatyā pujāsatkāram ārabhyaḥ cārturmahārājakāyikā devatā tathā | pūrvoktagandhadhūpapuṣpa [23\recto] ekaikakulise datvā paścād bhagavatyā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā prahasitamukhena mantram ucāryaḥ devā pūrve kīlita vijayet | sarvadevā vasyā bhavanti |
cchāyoṣṇakāla cakrapaṭaṃ bhāvyaṃ punar api sapta vārāṃ bhāvya adṛṣṭapaṭe ekapādena tiṣṭaṃ ādityaṃ nirīkṣayet | ekalakṣaṇe mūhūrtte yaṃ abhilasite vastu takṣaṇā siddhir bhavati | tasya ni[2]rīkṣitākṣi aśru pātayati tad aśru padmapatreṇa plavaiḥ gṛhya śuskāvartte tilaghṛtena pradīpaṃ prajvālya kapālasthitaḥ punar api karoṭikā upari sthita kajjalaṃ gṛhya asvasthapratre sthāpya punar api cakrapaṭaṃ sapta vārāṃ bhāvya adṛṣṭaṃ vātha ra<| ca |>vimaṇḍala mūrcchā ekakṣaṇamuhūrttaṃ tanmayīṃ bhāvayet | ekapādena tiṣṭaṃ tatkṣaṇā ravi <| kā |> [3] ākāse na dṛsyate ekatvam api cittayet mantradharaḥ || saha cittitamātreṇa siddhir bhavati | ākāse nakṣatraṃ dṛsyate | pūrvavat svayam eva nakṣatram upalakṣya adhivāsayet ṣaṭmukhamahāmudrāṃ badhvā tena jīvet | varṣasahasraṃ pañcamaṃ jarārogamṛtyuvinirmukta pālayanti nakṣatrā dine 2 mānavaḥ pūrvoktakajjalaṃ sarvā vasyasthāne [4] akṣiṇya prayojayet || parvatasikharam āruhya smasāna iṅgala suvarṇṇagairikāmisreṇa satrupratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā prāṅmukha sthātavyaḥ vāmapādena hṛdayasthāne avaṣṭabhya āditya nirīkṣya saptavārāṃ cakrapaṭa bhāvya sasarṣapeṇa śrutrupratikṛtiṃ mukhe hattavyaṃ | saha hanitamātreṇa satru mṛyate || pratyānayanaṃ karttavyaṃ | [5] taṃ pratikṛtiṃ cakrapaṭasyāgrataḥ ālikhya pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpacakrasya kulisaṃ kulisa bhagavatyā mūlamudrāmantrasahitā kṣīrasarṣapamisraṃ tatpratikṛtiṃ prakṣālayet | traya āṃjali pūrayitvā ādityaṃ saṃmukhaṃ pātayitvā amukaṃ śāṃti bhavatv iti vaktavyaṃ
% henceforth gulikās?
mūḍhagarbhamṛta<| ¯ |>bālakaṃ gṛhya paṭasyāgrataḥ caturṣu kuṇḍe smasā[23\verso]nakāṣṭair agni prajvālya aṣṭasataṃ juhuyāt samāpte caturṣu kuṇḍe bhasmaṃ gṛhya gugulu sahaikīkṛtya gulikāṃ kārayet māraṇe soṣaṇe ucāṭane vidveṣaṇe sarve sthāne nikhattavyaṃ saha nikhanitamātraś ca niyojitakarmeṇa pralayaṃ yānti | sa gṛhya sa kuḍrya sa prākāra sa grāmaḥ pratyānayanaṃ + ttavya uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ ||
catuviṃśa satru[2]pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā nisācare kṛṣṇapakṣe ca paṭasya sapta bhāvyaṃ ekā gulikāṃ tasya udare nikhatavyaṃ saha nikhanitamātra mahatā vyādhinā gṛhyate uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ ||
sapta gulikāṃ gṛhya mātṛsthāne nikhatavyaṃ saha nikhanitamātreṇa saptaśatayojanābhyantareṇa śatrava mahatā ucchvāsena śāntaḥpuraṃ gamiṣyanti | uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ ||
% NB: mātṛsthāna
pūrvoktaguli[3]kāṃ ekagulikāṃ gṛhya aṣṭasatavārāṃ cakrapaṭa bhāvyaṃ vināyakasyāgrataḥ nikhatavyaṃ vināyakasyāgrataḥ nikhatavyaṃ ṣaṭmukhamudrāṃ badhvā mantrasahita vināyakasya niyojayet | saha niyojitamātre svaganāntagataḥ satrava mariṣyanti | punar api śānti bhaviṣyati |
pūrvoktaguḍikātrayaṃ gṛhya paṭasyāṣṭottarasataṃ bhāvyaṃ mahā[4]devasya gṛhe nikhanet | ṣaṭmukhamukhamudrā badhvā mantrasahitā mahādevasya niyojayet | saha niyojitamātraḥ tasya ganāttargataḥ satravo mariṣyanti | śāntir bhavati ||
% NB: mahādevagṛha (gaṇa = śaivas?)
pūrvoktagulikāṃ ekaṃ gulikā guhya mahākālasya mukhe prakṣipya ṣaṭmukhamudrāṃ mantrasahitaṃ tasya daśadisaṃ niyojya | amukaṃ gṛhṇāpayeti vaktavyaṃ [5] saha uktamātraḥ mahatā jvareṇa gṛhṇāpayati | tasya mukhe gulikaṃ uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ ||
% NB: Mahākāla?
pūrvoktatrayagulikā gṛhītvā cakrapaṭasya sapta bhāvya ādityagṛhe nikhanet saha nikhanitamātra śatruṇāmaṃ gṛhya paścād bhagavatyā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā tasya niyojayet | satrava mariṣyanti | śāntir bhabhavati |
gulikā saptābhiḥ nadīpra[24\recto]taraṇakāle na<dyā/dyo> kṣipet prakṣipyamātraḥ siśumāraḥ kumbhīra makara maṇḍūka kacchapa ekamukhī dvimukhī jalasarpā nadyā tīrtha yojanasatābhyantareṇa sarvaduṣṭaprāṇakajātayo na prabhavanti |
pūrvoktagulikā sapta gṛhya cakrapaṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpyaḥ paṭa sapta dhyāyī ekatra gulikaṃ gṛhya phalavṛkṣe nikhaṃtavyaṃ saha nikhanitamā[2]tra niṣphalo bhavati | uddhṛte phalasamanvito bhavati |
aṣṭabhir ekatra gulikā gṛhya kṣetramadhye nikhane indrāsanir yathā patati viṭapas tathā uddhṛtyaḥ ||
pañcabhir ekatra gulikaṃ gṛhya nāgabalisthāne saha nikhanitamātreṇa granthi khaṇḍā patanti | uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ ||
caturbhir ekagulikāṃ gṛhya hastisālāyā nikhane saha nikhanitamātreṇa [3] hasti mṛyate ||
% NB contra elephant-stables
tribhir ekatra gulikāṃ gṛhya asvasālāyāṃ nikhane asvā mṛyaṃte ||
% NB contra horse-stables
dvi<tī>ya ekatra gulikaṃ gṛhya citragṛhamadhye nikhanet saha nikhanitamātreṇa parirekhā āyāti dhanaṃ ||
eka gulikaṃ gṛhya śayana(?)sthāne nikhanet saha nikhanitamātreṇa nidrā bhavati | uddhṛtena bodhayet ||
pūrvoktasiddha(?)paṭe caturṣu mahārājākāyi[4]kādevāpṛṣṭet caturnyāse upacāra prathamaṃ dhṛtarāṣṭrasya pṛṣṭe vajrāṃkuśasya upacāraṃ kathayiṣyāmi | pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpanivedyasiddhapaṭasya datvā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā mantram ucārya bhāvanāṃ aṃkuśavajraṃ sādhayāmīti cittayet <|> paścāt puṣpāṃjaliṃ bhagavatyā datvā cakrapaṭa dakṣiṇenaika bhāvyaḥ sarṣapena dhṛtarāṣṭrasya pṛṣṭe < > vajramudgaras tathā e[5]te caturṣu sthāneṣu sādhayet | ekaikaṃ nayuta bhāvyaḥ sarṣapeṇa tāḍayet saha tāḍita<| ¯ |>mātraiḥ <| dhṛtarāṣṭrasya |> | netrāśrū(?) payati | dṛṣṭ<vā> sādhakena tasya tasya uktagandhapuṣpadhūpamālyamatsamānsanivedya mayūraghṛtaḥ māṣakuśamiśrānyad api dviguṇaṃ datvā paścād dhastapādaḥ prakṣālya gandhopaliptahasta ta hastā vimukta dopi (?) | cakra vikramacakram vā [24\verso] mi (?) mahākṣepaṃ badhvā mantram ucārya<| ¯ |> caturṣu kulise niyojya caturṣu mahārājā sarvamaṇḍalasthā siddhi kariṣyanti |
pūrvoktacakrapaṭaḥ yathoktavidhinā asuravivarasthāne guhyena sthāpyaḥ pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpadīpamālyopahāra kuryāt dine 2 cakrapaṭaṃ bhāvayet saptataḥ aṣṭame divase yadi nāgacchati | trayatriṃsapriyadevamahākṣepaṃ mantrasa[2]hitaṃ asuravivare niyojayet saha niyojitamātraḥ asurakanyā āgatya sādhakaṃ gṛhya asuravivaraṃ pravesyanti | martyaloke bhilāsaṃ na karttavyaṃ ||
% NB bilasādhana
nidhisthāne cakrapaṭasya pūrvoktavidhinā sthāpya gandhapuṣpadhūpanivedyapradīpyamālyopahāryaḥ || paṭasya samaṃtāt pūrvoktamudrāmantrasahita datvā cakrapaṭa ekarātraṃ bhāvyaṃ nidhisthāne[3]ṣu mukhamudrāṃ badhvā sthāpayet saha sthāpitamātraḥ nidhiḥ dṛśyate yathāsukhena gṛhya bhoktavyam ||
ratnatraye dānābhirataḥ kṛpākāruṇyacetasa pratimakesasaṃ<| ¯ |>skārādibhirataiḥ sadharmalikhinapaṭhanasvādhyāyyanapūjāsartkarābhirataiḥ parityajya īrṣyāmātsaryamadopalepaiḥ dānapāramitā paripūrayitavyaḥ mantradhareṇa yathā hi [4] paṅkākumudaḥ aliptasūryaśaśigaganaivā anuttarāṃ samyaksaṃbodhiḥ prārthayaṃ tu śakyate mantradhareṇa vicakṣaṇaḥ
% vajrāṅkuśasādhanavidhi begins
pūrvoktacakrapaṭe catuṣkuṇḍa pūrva prathamakuṇḍeṣu ṣya lohamayīṃ vajrāṃkuśaviśvavajra hastapramāṇāṃ kṛtvā sulakṣaṇaiḥ suvarṇṇanulaiḥ daṇḍair aṣṭāśa<| u |>raṃkusair aṣṭāṃgulasupātanaiḥ aṃkusastūpasthāne aṃkusāgraiḥ kakāraṃ vinyaset | [5] madhye ākāraṃ vinyaset tatraivāṃkuśamūlaparve śīkāraṃ nyaset daṇḍāgre vajra pañcasūcīṃ krodhavajra madhyamasūcīṃ bhrūṃkāraṃ tatraiva sūcīṃ dvitīyāṃ sūcīṃ hrīkāraṃ nyaset | tatraiva sūcīṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ oṃkāraṃ nyaset | tatraiva caturthyā sūcyā āḥkāraṃ nyaset madhyamā sūcīṃ caturdise sūcyāṃ madhyaparve hūṃkāraṃ nyaset dvitīyasūcyā hūṃ[25\recto]kāraṃ nyaset dvitīyasūcā hūṃkāraṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ sūcyās tathā hūṃkāraṃ caturthasūcyā hūṃkāra daṇḍai trayasthāne vajraṃ nyaset | caturaṃgulapramāṇaṃ pūrvoktavajro aṣṭāṃgulānte daṇḍa caturaṃgulavajraṃ trisūcīṃ nyaset tatraiva daṇḍamadhye vinyaset | tatraiva daṇḍe madhye viśvavajraṃ nyaset | ekāṃgulāntare nisevetaiḥ samastaiḥ samāpte raktacanda[2]nasalilaṣarpayamiśreṇa vajrāṃkuśaṃ śnāpayet pūrvoktasiddhipaṭa yathoktavidhinā guhyasthāne sthāpya pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ datvā paṭasyāgrataḥ asvasthapatraiḥ pūrvoktavajrāṃkuśa sthāpya punar api gandhapuṣpadhūpan datvā bhagavatyā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā mantram ucārya dvāśasire ekaikasire mudrāmantraṃ sahitaṃ darśayet punar api mudrāmantrasa[3]hitaṃ aṣṭādaśāyudhe adhitiṣṭet paścā svetaṃ hi piṣpaṃ svetaṃ hi candanaṃ sveta nivedyaṃ ghṛtaṃ pradīpaṃ vajraṃkuśe datvā bhavatyā vajrāṃkuśas tathā datvā paścād vajrāṃkusaṃ sādhayet | trayaḥkulāṃtargataḥ caturmahāmudrā ātmānaṃ niyojayet ||

sādhu sādhu vidyādharendra namaskṛtya ekānte sthitaḥ pūrvavidyādharapurādhipatya vidyātkṛtpaṭaṃ nāma vidyādharā uvāca | asthe [3] vidyādharendra vidyādharapure amṛtaṃ praṇīta pañcakāmaguṇaṃ vajrāṃkuśa vidyāradharendreṇa m-etad avocat śṛṇuṣva vidyādhara purādhipatya nayā anekajarme anubhuktakāmaguṇaḥ rājyasvahariharahiraṇyagarbhaisvaryatva mayānubhuktā brahmendratva tathā | pacān tarakatiryakpreta anekakalpakoṭīniyutāni ca mayā taptāḥ kharāḥ kaṭukāḥ [4] vedayantaḥ sasāre duḥkhā acintiyāḥ śrutvā vidyādharapurādhipatya sādhu 2 ekabhūmiprāptakalpakā bodhisatvā anekavidyādharakoṭīniyutaśataśahasre vidyādharakaṇyā nayutaśatasahasrāṇi ca | sarve te ekasvapariṇirghoṣeṇa ca svareṇa tam āmaṃtrayati sma | iha janme ajarāmaratva ratnair vābhiṣikta lokottaravādi vajrāṃkuśa[5]vidyādharendraḥ pratiṣṭā vidyādharapuraḥ dṛṣṭvā vidyādharakanyā anekāni nayutasatasahasrāṇi pañcakāmaguṇair api mantāṃ krīḍaṃtāṃ ttaḥ puraṃ dṛṣṭvā vajrāṃkuśavidyādharendra a<| nu |>bhaṃ adhruvaṃ mūtrapurīṣabhājanakimi ø ø ø ø ø ø ø kūṭabhūtaṃ pittaṣleṣmānilayam āsṛtya karībhāra<| ¯ |>ṃ mṛtaṃ kṣipra smasāna yathā kāṣṭabhūtaṃ śrutvā vidyādharapurādhipatyaṃ praru[26\verso]dantaṃ anekavidyādhara koṭīniyutaśatasahasram etad avocat

samanaṃtara vajrākuśa vidyādharendraḥ trayatriṃsa vigalitapriyavaca mahākṣepaṃ badhvāḥ matram ucāryaḥ pūrve vajravijṛṃbhaṇaṃ nāma mahāvajre dhiṣṭitaṃ saha dhiṣṭitamātraḥ vidyādharaḥ pura nivāśinya adṛśyena baddhapa[4]ṭakakṣa vajrāṃkusapānināṃ atiḥ caturmudrāmaṇḍaleḥ catuḥsamādhicakrabhāvane vajrāṃkuśasādhanavidyādharaḥ

% vajraparaśusādhanavidhi begins
athāta pūrvoktasiddhapaṭe vajraparśusādhanopacāravidhi kathayiṣyāmiḥ apūrvokta ekatra sthāne gṛha saskārya mṛdayāvaliptaḥ sa[5]gāḍhaiḥ kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇacaturdasyāṃ ahorātroṣito bhūtvā maṃtradhareṇa pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpanivedyapradīpamālyopahāryaḥ svaisve mantramudrāsahitaṃ daśadiśaṃ datvā disābandha prākāra paṃjaraṃ kīla dharaṇībandhaḥ || ātmana sve sve karme pada saṃyogyaḥ sumantritaḥ puṣpalohamayaṃ vajraparaśu sotriṃsāgulapramāṇaṃ aṣṭaṃgulapa[27\recto]raśudvaraṃ tatraiva paraśu dīrghāṣṭāṃgulavajrapara aṣṭāṃguladaṇḍāgre padmasthite trisūcikrodhavajraṃ daṇḍasulakṣaṇaiḥ suvattalaiḥ paraṣu daṇḍa sauviśanti aṃgulaṃ te vajrakīla ekaṃgula etaiḥ samastaiḥ parasumadhye oṃ āḥ hrīḥ etet paraśujihve nyāsaḥ daṇḍasyāgre vakṣe madhyamāsūcī sṛṃkāra nyaset dvitīyasūcī sakāraṃ [2] nyaset vajrakīle kāraṃ nyaset samāpte svetacandanasalilasugandhapuppamiśreṇa vajraparasuṃ śnāpyaḥ punar api sugandhakarpūmiśra vajraparaśuṃ avalipyaḥ vibhītakīsamudgake prakṣipyaḥ pūrvoktasiddhapaṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpya gandhapuṣpadhūpasiddhapaṭasya datvā cārturmahārājakāyikā devās tathā paścāt trayaḥkulaśāmā[3]nyacartumudrāṃ badhvā mantram ucāryaḥ ātmanaṃ caturdise niyojayet || caturmahākṣepas tathā paścā bhagavatyā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā maṃtram ucāryaḥ caturṣu vajra niyojayet cāturmahārājakāyikā devās tathā | paścād bhāgavatyā vajraparaśu sarvaduṣṭaḥsiracchedaṃ sādhayāmīti cittayet ||
punar api gandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ siddhapaṭasya [4] datvā siddhaṃ paṭaṃ ekā bhāvya ṣarṣapeṇa pūrvoktasamudgakaṃ tāḍayet | anenaiva prakāreṇa ekalakṣaṇaṃ bhāvayet | atha lakṣe yadi ūṣmāyati samudgaṃ haritāla asurī gugulumisraṃ dhṛtarāṣṭrasya pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā dvādaśāṃgulapramāṇaṃ sarvālaṃkāravibhūṣitaṃ pūrvoktavajraṃkuśadaṇḍavajra sādhakasyānte paṭābhimukhaṃ sthāpya [5] kunaṭikūparpāsānaś cūrṇṇa | sikṣakamisraṃ virūḍhakasya pratikṛti kṛtvā dvādaśāṃgulapramāṇaṃ sarvālaṃkāravibhūṣitaṃ vajraparaśudaṇḍe vajrasādhakasyāntare paṭasyābhimukhaṃ sthāpayita<| ¯ |>vyaḥ kapilāgomayaṃ samudragāminī nadyā ubhayakūlamṛtyā asurīkagavitamisraṃ virūpākṣasya pratikṛti kṛtvā dvādasāṃ[27\verso]lapramāṇaṃ sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitaṃ navaphaṇitamaṇicūrṇṇaṃ pūrvoktavajrapāśaṃ kuṇḍe vajrasādhakasyottare tatpratikṛti pūrvoktacartuṣu pratimaṃ gṛhya sve sve dise mukhaṃ sthāne sthāpayitavyaḥ yadi abhavitavyaḥ raudrakarmārabhet caturṣu mahāmudrāṃ badhvā mantram ucārya ātmānaṃ sapta 2 vārāṃ caturdisaṃ niyojayet || kiṃ mayā karttavyam iti kathayati | sa rājñaḥ vidyādhareṇa catuṣu kuṇḍe agnikāryaṃ asamāptena vaktavyam | tatkṣaṇād vajraparaśujvālāmālavinirgataḥ ubhaya tiṣṭanti (((vajraparaśu dvividhā karma dvitīya virūḍhaka siddhapaṭasyāntare kuṇḍaḥ apāmārga[5]samidbhir agni prajvālyaḥ pūrvoktakapālasthitarudhirasarṣapa tatpratikṛtiṃ chitvā 2 rudhirasarṣapamisraṃ siddhapaṭa ekabhāvyaḥ nayutaṃ juhuyāt | tatkṣaṇā virūḍhako mahārājaḥ āgataḥ kiṃ kruddhasādhaka varaṃ brūhi | sādhaka mama bhagavana dagdhanisphalā savilayagataḥ tataḥ kiṃ mayā karttavyaḥ vidyādhareṇa ka[28\verso]thayati || devāḥ vidyādhareṇa vajrapāśamahāmudrā badhvā mantram ucāryaḥ tasya rājñe pañca pāsena bandhayet tataḥ pūrvapṛthivīsthitavajraparaśuṃ aṣṭāṃgula ubhayāntarikṣe tiṣṭhanti | punar api hastapāda prakṣālya gandhapuṣpadhūpa bhagavatyā siddhapaṭe vajraparaśus tathā ||

caturtha kuverasya siddhapaṭasyāntare kuṃḍe devadārukāṣṭair agni prajvālyaḥ pūrvoktakapālasthitarudhirami[4]sraṣarṣapataḥ (?) tatpratikṛtiṃ sthitvā rudhirasarṣapar ekīkṛtya siddhapaṭe ekabhāvyaḥ aṣṭasata juhuyāt vajrapāninā saha hanitamātraḥ kuverasya mahārājaḥ hā hā kaṣṭaṃ dagdhāmamasarīraṃ kiṃ kurvorundhaṃ vidyādharaḥ varaṃ brūhi sādhakaḥ iti kathayati | sa rājña vidyādhareṇa hastau prakṣālya vikṛtajvālamahāmudrāṃ [5] badhvā mantram ucāryaḥ yakṣarājaṃ pañca pāsena bandhayet saha bandhitamātraḥ pūrva soḍasāṃgula ubhayāntarīkṣe sthitaḥ vajraparaśuṃ punar api dvaṣṭiraṃgulapramānaṃ ubhayāṃtarīkṣe sthitaḥ āviṣṭa bhramati siddhaḥ vajraparasuṃ ekatra sthāne preṣayitavyaḥ saha preṣitamātraḥ tasyā siraṃ schedayitvā śīghraṃ aparāgacchati | pūrva[29\recto]sthitasthāne rudhiramrakṣitasiddhavajraparaśuṃ tiṣṭati dṛṣṭvā siddhaṃ vajraparaśuṃ | hastapādaṃ prakṣālyaḥ hastena hasta gandhāvalipta puṣpāṃjaliṃ pūrayitvā sarvābuddhasamāyogastūparatnakaraṇḍaka datvā paścāt | bhagavatyā mūlamudrāṃ badhvā mantram ucāryaḥ bhagavatyā siddhapaṭe kulisakulise adhiṣṭya cāturmahārāji[2]kā devās tathā

pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpa yathāvidhiṃ datvā paścā siddhavajraparaśuṃ gṛhyaḥ sugandhasalilasarṣapamisreṇa | tat siddhapararśu śnāpayet | agaru dhūpayitvā śītaharikapūrakolalavaṃga tathā etāni samabhāgāni pīṣayitvā cavāriṇyataṃ gatvānuliptasiddhavajraparaśuṃ nīlavastreṇa veṣṭya siddhapaṭasyā[3]grataḥ sthātavyaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe bhiḥmārakarma paśya īṣyā tasya nāma gṛhyaḥ siddhapaṭaṃ sapta bhāvyaḥ vajraparaśuṃ pāṇinā paścād vajraparaśuṃ aṃtarīkṣe prakṣipyaḥ eka dvikaṃ trikaṃ siracchedayam iti preṣaṇīya saha preṣitamātraḥ uktasirahataḥ śīghra āgaccha vidyādharaḥ lalāṭe antīrīkṣe tiṣṭhati | vidyādhareṇa [4] tasmiṃ siddhavajraparaśu gṛhya dvitīya namaskṛtyaḥ pūrvoktavidhinā śnāpayitvāvaliptaḥ pūrvoktavastreṇa veṭayitvā siddhapaṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpayet anenaiva vajraprākāreṇa sarvaduṣṭasatvānāṃ vajraparaśuṃ preṣayitayaḥ rājā vā rājāpramadā vā rājyamantriṇya vā rājamantripramadā vā brahmaṇya brāhmaṇapramadā [5] vā | vaiśyā vā vaiṣyapramadā vā vṛṣalā vā vṛṣalī vā anekaduṣṭa nānāprakāra bahuvidhā pūrvoktavajraparaśuṃ siddhaṃ paṭa sastabhāvyaḥ bhāvyaḥ yatra yatra preṣitaḥ preṣitakārya niṣpādya śīghraṃ āgacchati | vidyādharaḥ lalāṭe antarīkṣe tiṣṭhanti | siddhavajraparaśuṃ gṛhyaḥ pūrvokta śnānālepanatattaiḥ ukta[29\verso]vastreṇa veṣṭayitvā siddhapaṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpayitavyaḥ etad dṛṣṭapreṣaṇasiracchedaṃ tatsiddhavajraparaśuṃ sarvaduṣṭasthānapraveṣaṇaṃ vidyādharavajraparaśupāṇinā kathayiṣyāmi | pūrvoktavidhinā siddhapaṭa sapta bhāvyaḥ vajraparaśupāṇi adṛsye ta satru antarīkṣe prakṣipya ṣaṭmukhamudrā badhvā pracchāditavastre [2] mantram ucāryaḥ rājā sāntaḥpuraḥ dṛso bhavati | mahatā krodhābhibhūtā bhāvita tatkṣaṇa mukhastaṃbhitā bhaviṣyanti | pūrvokta antarīkṣam uktavajraparaśu gṛhya paraśupāṇihastānāgatavyaṃ | siddhavajraparaśuṃ paṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpayitavyaḥ pūrvoktagandhāvalepreḥ dine 2 smarayitavyaḥ

atha vajrapāsasādhanopacāraḥ pravakṣyāmi sarvānyadevatā laukikā iti bahuvidhā nānāprakārabhedabhedā sve sve saṃtuṣṭā satvā iti kathayanti nānālaukikamantradhareṇa dīkṣaṃ datvā satvagocara iti kathayanti mūḍho laukikadṛṣṭiḥ [4] vajrapāseṇa sarvalaukikalokotaradevā pāsena kīlayataḥ uccāṭanaṃ prāṅmukhīkṛtaṃ | vajrapāsayet |
pūrvoktasiddhapaṭasyāgrataḥ atha tāvat pūrvoktavidhinā gṛhyam āra<| ba |>bdha suyantraghaṭitaḥ bhūmyāṃ sulakṣaṇaiḥ pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpai gṛhasamatāṃ datvā pūrvoktamantramudrāsahitaṃ disābandhaprā[5]kārakīlapaṃjara āsanas tathā paścā siddhapaṭan ta gṛhya sthāpyaḥ yathokta bhagavatyā gandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ datvā trayaḥ kulasāmānyaḥ caturmahāmudrāṃ badhvā mucāryaḥ ātmanasya catudise niyojayet pañcaraṃgikasūtreṇa dvātriṅśadaṃgulipramāṇaṃ ekaikabhāgamadhye aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇaṃ trisūcīpuṣpalo[30\recto]hamayī vajro madhyamasūcī prākāraṃ nyaset | tatraiva madhyamā sūcī oṃkāraṃ nyaset | tasya vāmasūcī hrīkāraṃ nyaset | tasya dakṣiṇasūcī ṭhakāraṃ nyaset tatra madhyadvitīyā madhyamasūcī prākāraṃ nyaset | tasya vāmasūcī hūṃkāraṃ nyaset tasyaiva dakṣiṇasūcīṃ bhṛṃkāraṃ nyaset etaiḥ samastaiḥ nyāsaiḥ [2] samāpte gandhodakasarṣapamisreṇa sugandhapuṣpasahitaṃ vajrapāśāṃ śnāpya asvatthasamudga ekahastapramāṇaṃ tad vajrapāśāṃ tatsamudge prakṣipya pūrvoktasiddhapaṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpya paṭe ekabhāvya sarṣapena tāḍayet | tatsamudga anenaiva prakāreṇa trisaṃdhā nayutaṃ bhāvayec cakrapaṭaṃ yadi nayutāṃ tenoṣmāpayi[3]ti samudgaṃ punar api nayuta paṭa ekabhāvya paṭasya cāturmahārājakāyikā sarṣapeṇa tāḍayet sahi tāḍitamātraḥ pūrvoktasamudgaṃ uṣmāpayati | mantradhareṇa tatsamudgaṃ na spṛśitavyaṃ || punar api gandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ datvā siddhapaṭe ekabhāvya sarṣapena tāḍayet | yāvaj jināni nayutāṃ te tad vajrapāśaṃ [4] jvālāmālavinirgataḥ ubhayata tiṣṭhanti | dṛṣṭvā siddha mahāvajrapāśa mahatā udāreṇa pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpadīpamālyopaharaṃ daśadisaṃ datvā siddhapaṭe cāturmahārājakāyikā devās tathā paścāt sugandhapuṣpadhūpapradīpa siddhavajrapāse datvā svetavastreṇa pracchādya tatsiddhapaṭasyāgrataḥ sthāpayi[5]tavyaḥ siddhavajrapāse upacāra mahardhikaṃ mahānubhāvaṃ mahatejaṃ vajrapāsa saṃsāramohapāśaṃ baddhabahusatvāpīḍitaṃ jaga rogadaurmanasya bhavate śuklaṃ śuptaḥ khādanti dantena dantaṃ akālanindrāṃ paśyanti | svapne asubhaṃ ghoraṃ kṛṣikarme phalaṃ na bhavanti vidyānāvarmamya (?) bahusaṭhā sthitaḥ uktaḥ ubhayataḥ [30\verso] mohena etaiḥ avaṣṭadya satvaiḥ vighnena cchiddhita iti vidyādhareṇa etādṛśā aśubha upalakṣa tasya pratikṛti svete saha tāḍitamātraḥ pañcāṇaṃtaryakāriṇya api subha pāsamukto bhavati | tatpuruṣa gurudakṣiṇyaṃ dātavyaṃ ||
mantreṇa mantraṃ bandhayitum icchati sādhakaḥ svakīya 2 tasya pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā yasya tantre abhiyuktasya prāṅmukhīkarttavyaḥ mahādevasya pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā velyakāṣṭam vā nimbakāṣṭaṃ vā vinsati aṅgula[3]pramāṇaṃ sarvālaṃkāravibhūṣitaṃ trinetraṃ caturbhujaṃ pralaṃbitajaṭāmukuṭaṃ dakṣiṇavāmopari hastakṛtakarapuṭo aṃjali dakṣiṇadvitīyahasta akṣasūtra vāmadvitīyahaste trisūlaṃ tatpratikṛtiṃ gṛhya siddhapaṭasyābhimukhaṃ sthāpyaḥ gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpa siddhapaṭasya datvā cāturmahārājakāyikā devā siddhavajra[4]pāśas tathā ātmarakṣevataḥ mahāmudrā niyojyaḥ paścān mahādevatantrābhiyuktaḥ ekāṃ tāṃ puruṣanāsaṃ gṛhya mantraṃ kīlayāmi iti cintayitvā pūrvoktavajrapāsāṃ gṛhya tatpratikṛti pāsayet | siddhapaṭaḥ ekabhāvya sarṣapeṇa amukaṃ prāṅmukhī tiṣṭhanti tāḍayet saha tāḍitamātre sahāntakrodhena prāṅmukha[5]nti yāva<| ī |>jjīvaṃ ca ||
pratikṛtiṃ niṣpādya śnāna mṛnyoḥ mukha sthāpya ne sthāpya utkīlayet | siddhapaṭa ekabhāvya āmukhībhavati rudraḥ smasānabhasmaṃ gṛhya sarṣapamisraṃ mahādevasya pratikṛtiṃ dvātriṃśadaṅgulapramāṇaṃ siddhapaṭasyāgrataḥ tam ālikhya jaṭāmakuṭabaddha kapālārddhacandraṃ trinetraṃ ṣaḍbhujaṃ sarvālaṃ[31\recto]kāravibhūṣitaṃ dakṣiṇavāmoparihastena namaskāraṃ dakṣiṇadvitīyahasta akṣasūtraṃ tatraiva tṛtīyahaste trisūlaṃ vāmadvitīya kunsāśaṃ tatraiva tṛtīyahaste kapālaṃ anenaiva prakāreṇa tam ālikhya gandhapuṣpadhūpasiddhapaṭasya datvā cāturmahārājakāyikā devās tathā paścā caturmahāmudrāṃ ba[2]dhvā mantram ucāryaḥ | ātmakrakṣe niyojayet mahādevasya mantra badhvāmīti cittayitvā ekatvapuruṣaṇāmaṃ gṛhyaḥ mahādevasya pratikṛtiṃ vāmapādenāvaṣṭabhya mantradharaiḥ trayatriṃsapriyadevasya mahākṣepaṃ badhvā vajrapāśāṃ bāhunā mantram uccāryaḥ athakṣāpañcadasa samādhinā | avaṣṭhadya pādenāvaṣṭhabhyaḥ [3] eka vārāṃ siddhapaṭabhāvya tatpratikṛti sarṣapeṇa tāḍayet ubhayamantradharaḥ yuṣmatantrābhiyuktasya amukam abhiyuktasya mantra kīlayāmīti vaktavyaḥ sahobhrūmātraḥ mahatā uṣmaphala āsannasiddhi niḥphala yāyanti āgataḥ śītaḥ āgata śīta kaṃputāvaṣṭavya śītena ucyante akaṃpitadeha ā[4]gataśīta ity ucyate | gataḥ kuta gatiḥ ka bhayaṃ āgataḥ kīdṛsā rūpeṇāvaṣṭadyaḥ etāvatā dṛśāvalokita maṃtradharaḥ alakṣāvaṣṭadyaḥ śīte jalasthāna mahatā yāyanti tādṛśā saptajanmāntaraiḥ tataḥ pratikṛtiṃ bhasmaṃ gṛhyaḥ raktavastraṃ badhvā siddhapaṭa sapta bhāvyaḥ catuḥpathe nikhantavyaḥ saha ni[5]khanitamātra sadya antarahito bhavaṃti |

athātaḥ lokottaramantreṇa lokottaramantrabandhanaṃ | lokottaramaṃtreṇa lokottaramantrakīlaṃnaṃ etat | etad aśādhyaṃ armiskandhaṃ dakyatte bhave [4] bhavāntaḥ || mantreṇa mantrabandhanaṃ ca kīlayet | na ca parāṅmukhaṃ kārayet | vidyādevā yadi lokottaramantreṇa lokottaramantrakīlanabandhanm icchati sādhakaḥ

prathamaṃ tathāgatakulamantramudrābhedaḥ ākṣepaḥ tathā uttamamadhyamakaniṣṭha tṛvidhaṃ maṃtra tatra ku[5]lāṃtargata trividhimudrāḥ uttamamadhyamaniṣṭamudrāḥ ākṣepa trividhaḥ tatraivākṣepaḥ utamamadhyamkaniṣṭaṃ tathā | tatraiva mantramudrākṣepaḥ trividhā siddhiḥ utamamantre uttamā siddhiḥ utamamadhyamaḥ utamamadhyamasiddhiḥ utamakaniṣṭhāḥ utamakaniṣṭasiddhiḥ madhyama utamaḥ madhyamamadhyama | madhyamakaniṣṭaḥ <hīnotamaḥ> hīnamadhya[32\verso]maḥ hīna<| ṣṭa |>kaniṣṭhaḥ utamamadhyamakaniṣṭa utkṛṣṭamadhyamakaniṣṭasiddhiḥ
ekaṃ tathāgatakulamantrasiddhiphalaṃ suvyaktaṃ labdhopadeśakaḥ bhinnabhedapadārtha upalakṣya mantradharaḥ tathāgatakulān tathāgatakulāntargatavidyādevatāḥ bandhanakīlanabhedabhedanaṃ jinaurasānāṃ || maitreyakumārabhṛte ayakarma samārabhet samā[2]dhiṃ svetacandanakāṣṭena dvibhujyaṃ dvādaśāṃgulapramāṇaṃ jaṭāmakuṭasthitacaityaṃ sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitaṃ dakṣiṇavāma uparihastena tathāgatanamaskṛtākṛtiṃ | anenaiva prakāreṇa vajrahasta vajrapāni padmahastaṃ padmadharas tathā | ākāśagarbha khaḍgahastas tathā māniradūpaścittā manihastaḥ kṣitigarbhaḥ kṣitihastaḥ [3] kṣitihastas tathā viskambhiṇa visvahasteṃ tathā maṃjusriyaṃ prajñāpāramitālikhitapustakahastaḥ etaiḥ samastaiḥ bodhisatvaiḥ saṃkṣepataḥ dvibhujaiḥ samādhiṃ yasya kasyacit bhikṣubhikṣuṇyupāsakopāsikā tasyābhiyuktasya mantrasamayaṃ suvyaktena maitrākumāraprabhṛtaiḥ ekatra samādhi pūrvoktavidhinā uktakāṣṭe[4]na saskārya sulakṣaṇeḥ akhaṇḍakarṣakeṣeḥ padmasthitaḥ jinaurasaṃ samāpte svetacandanakalpikavārimisraṃ śānpya tatpratimaṃ punar api sugandhagandhāpuṣpāgarudhūpaṃ tatpratime datvā trayaḥkulasāmānyamahāmudrāmantrasahitaṃ badhvā ātmānaṃ daśadisaṃ niyojya paścā puṣpāṃjali pūrayitvā sākyamunes tathāgatasya [5] datvā tatpratikṛtiṃ gṛhya sugandhadhūpādhivāsitaḥ vastra veḍhayitvā svayam eva devakāryakṛtasthāna sthāpyaḥ yāvat pūrṇṇamāsyā āsannadivaśa pūrvoktavidhinā gṛha saṃskārya mṛdayā lipta sugāḍhaiḥ sulakhaṇasarvadoṣavimuktayuktabhūmau bhagavatyā ākṣepaṃ badhvā mantram ucārya tasair (p.c., tasya? mantradhareṇa tasya lokasyārthapratyāyana kartum icchati vidyādhareṇa vajraśrītejagarbhamahāmaṇḍale uktavidhinā mocāpayati [curl] [2] caturthadivase yadi nāmocāpayi nodhṛyati tasyāntargataviṣaye sadyāśani pātayati
pratyānayanam kartukāma sādhakaḥ pūrvoktavajraśrītejagarbhamaṇḍalam adhiṣṭhānasya m-uktavidhinā atha nistaṃbhayet || pañcamadivase yadi nodhṛyate | kalpādāham upajāte jaṃbūdvīpe harihara|hyaraṃṇyagarbhādayaḥ hā hā [3] kaṣṭaṃ hi kaṣṭaṃ jaṃbūdvīpe dagdha niṣphalāsaḥ kṛtakarapuṭā sādhakasya kathayanti devāḥ
sādhakena pratyānayana karttam icchatā | asya mantre catumudrāmaṇḍale uktakarmeṇa kalpādāhaḥ upasāṃtavya iti |
% vidyādevīs in tathāgatakula: prajñāpāramitā, sarvabuddhadharmagalī, āryakāpālī, somebody, sarvabuddhanetrī, mahāmāyurī, maitryā, ānandā; their effigy is made
sarvasatva upasantapitā tathāgatakule vidyādevyāḥ prajñāpāramitā sarvabuddhadharmagalī āryakāpālī sta[4]vasaviddhanau sarvabuddhanetrī mahāmāyūrīs tathā maitryā cānandāṃs tathā etā vidyādevyāḥ anuttara|mahāpadaprāptaḥ vineyarūpā tasya pāsva vidyādevyā |
