vidyujvālākarālī pretotkaṭaguhyamaṇḍalaṃ ||
athātaḥ bhagavatyā mudrālakṣaṇaṃ bhavati
atha dvayoḥ ha<s>taḥ candanena mrakṣayitvā ciṭi 2 kā vimuktakaradvayo dvau hastau añjaliṃ badhvā hṛdayasthāne sthāpya<ḥ> [4] jyeṣṭāṃguṣṭhaṃ anyonyaṃ pāsayet | kaniṣṭābhyaṃtare vaktavyaḥ anāmikādvayaṃ kuṃḍalākṛti tarjanyāṃ tathaiva ca | madhyamā sūcīdvayaṃ prasārayet sarvajñena sākyamuninā ūrṇṇākośās sunirmirtā iti draṣṭavyaṃ | caturāsītidharmaskandha iti praṣṭavyaḥ sākṣā saptabhibuddhanetrīnirmitā iti mudrā draṣṭavyāḥ | svetavijṛṃbhaṇavajrī sarvakarmakarī [5] pretotkaṭaṃ nāma mudrā ayaṃ mantraḥ hūṃ hūṃ hrī phaṭ 2 | vajrī satvakarmakarī svāhā || pūrvoktamaṇḍalapretotkaṭaṃ vetāḍaṃ maṇḍale sarvamaṇḍale prayojayet | śīmābandha dha<ra>nībaṃ kīla prākāra paṃjaraṃ nyāsaṃ āsanenaiva etenaiva prakāreṇa pūrvoktena pretotkaṭamahāmaṃḍale anena mantreṇa karttavyaḥ
anenaiva prakāreṇa dvau hastau aṃjali[47\recto]kṛtā jeṣṭhadvayaṃ guhyasthāne aṃkuśākāraṃ tasyopari madhyamādvayaṃ gāḍhena pāsayet | jeṣṭhadvayāgre kaniṣṭhadvayaṃ kuṇḍalākṛtī anāmikādvayaṃ anyonya prasārya tarjanīdvayena anāmikāsūcī bandhayet || vajravijṛṃbhaṇakrodhotkaṭamahāmudrā || ayam mantraḥ oṃ raudrapracaṇḍavegadhara prahara 2 sarvaduṣṭvāṃ hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ 2 svāhā | ane[2]na mudrāmantreṇa pūrvadigbhāge pūrvābhimukhaṃ sapta vārāṃ prayojayet | ayaṃ prayojitamātreṇa saptayojanāśatamahāśīmābandha kṛto bhavati | ayaṃ mantra mudrā sapta vārāṃ ātmāna viyojayet | evaṃ dakṣiṇa paścima uttarāyāṃ pūrvoktena mudrāmantreṇa karttavya
dvau hastau anyonyavajramuṣṭiṃ badhvā madhyamāsūcīṃ prasārya prahasi[3]tavajramuṣṭimahāmudrā | anena mudrayā ayaṃ mantraḥ oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrīḥ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā || iti paścimadvāre niyojayet | sapta vārāṃ niyojitamātreṇa samāhitā yojanaśatatrayaṃ mahāsīmābandha kṛto bhavati ||
anenaiva prakāreṇa dvau hastāv u<| bhavati |>bhayataḥ jeṣṭadvayena kaniṣṭadvayaṃ madhyamasūcīṃ pṛsve pāsayet | tarjanyādvayaṃ anānmi[4]kādvayaṃ prabodhayet | madhyamasūcīdvayaṃ anyonyapasāra tarjanyādvayaṃ anāmikādvayaṃ caturdise prabodhayet | trailokyavikramavajravijṛṃbhana ṣaṭmukhā nāma mahāmudrā | ayaṃ mantraḥ oṃ aḥ hūṃ hūṃ sūṃ sūṃ nāṃ hūṃ phaṭ 2 svāhā | pūrvadvāradigbhāge prayojayet | pratotkaṭamahāmaṃḍale sarvakarmāṇi raudrāhuti pravatteta sarvakarmakarī tathā[5]gatakule traḥyakulaḥ sāmānya sarvamaṃḍalasthāne prayojyaḥ || o ||
mudrāpaṭala samāptaḥ || o ||
athātaḥ | bhagavatyā mantrapaṭalaṃ vyākhyāsyāmi || tadyathā oṃ raudrini prakupitacaṃḍavegadhari vidyujihvā mahodara raudīkarpālamāladhari jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍita nānāpraharaṇadhari hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ 2 tathāgatasamayamantra smara ru[47\verso]ru 2 hūṃ hūṃ pramardaya sarvasatrūṇāṃ sonita piba pracaṇḍavegadhari paca pācaya tapa tāpaya hūṃ vikira 2 hūṃ daha 2 sarvasatrūṃ sarvaduṣṭaṃ nikṛta prasādaya hūṃ mahāvegavati kāla 2 hūṃ phaṭ 2
yam icchati vaktavya
ayaṃ mūlamantraḥ oṃ raudrini hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā ||
anena mantrane paṭasyāgrata ekalakṣaṇaṃ japet |
guguludhūpaṃ dahatā rakta[2]karavīra raktapānaṃ sitaṃ dātavyaṃ āgacchā 2 raudrini buddhasatya mātikrama<| ¯ |> saṃghasatya mātikramaḥ gandhapuṣpadhūpabali prayacchatāṃ mama kāryaṃ kuru ayam āvāhanamantraḥ ādyavaṣāṇe ṣaṭmukhaṃ āsanaṃ niyojayet | oṃ prasphuṭa 2 hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ jvālāvatī mahorasya gaccha 2 mahārākṣasi puṣpāvakīrṇṇa punar āgamanaṃ kuru | preṣaṇamantraḥ oṃ hrī hūṃ svā[3]hā |
puṣpamantraḥ oṃ suru 2 hūṃ svāhā |
dhūpamantraḥ oṃ raudra pracaṇḍa hūṃ śrīḥ svāhā |
nivedyamantraḥ oṃ jvalita huru 2 hūṃ svāhā |
pradīpamantraḥ oṃ hrīḥ kuru ravaiḥ hūṃ svāhā ||
baliyena parityāgamantraḥ oṃ namo bhagavati hilike tilike bhūtamāta paṭale hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ yam icchati taṃ vaktavyaḥ
tasyaiva ṣaṭmukhaṃ niyojayet | oṃ raudrapracaṃ[4]javegadhari prahara 2 sarvaduṣṭāṃ hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ 2 svāhā | yam icchati taṃ vaktavyaḥ
pūrvadakṣiṇakoṇe yakṣiṇyā mantraḥ tasyā upacāraḥ pūrvoktena vidhinā karttavyaḥ dakṣiṇapaścima uttaraṃ vā asyā mantrena karttavya
vidyujvālākarālīsvedāmbujyamahārākṣasīmantrapaṭalaḥ samāptaḥ || o ||
athāta dvau hasto hastena gatvāvalipta dvomabhi bandha sthā[5]ne aṃguṣṭena gopa yojayet | catuṣadviviṭi 2 kāviraktakaraṃ punanarapi candranena mrakṣayitvā dakṣiṇahastena vajramuṣṭiṃ badhvā aṣṭhādaśāyudhaṃ cittayet | hūṃkāraṃ ucārya vajramuṣṭi svayam eva lalāṭe nyaset | tatraiva dakṣiṇākṣī cāma dakṣiṇabāhūṃ nyāsayeta vāmabāhūṃ hṛdayasthāne tathaiva caḥ | bhagavatyā vajramuṣṭhimahā<| ba |>mudrā  [48\recto] sarvathāne ānmi prayojayet | tatraiva vajramuṣṭi badhvāḥ ekaikasthāne śūnyagrahe praviṣṭā manra pramaṃto vā usthiṣṭho vā tanmuṣṭhi sikhā sapta vārāṃ bhrāmayet |
athātaḥ akṣayā bhavati | dvau hastau anyonyasikhāṃtā atikrāṃta ūrdhadarśa pala vā  anyonyavidhanā vā vāmapāda purastākramya dakṣiṇapāda dvau bhrāmasya purastākramya ubhayasirā[2]tikrānta daśabala anyonya vilokyitiḥ bāhye veṣṭayeṣṭa dvayāgula lalāṭe nyaset dvekoparikā ekā eka sūtreṇa dhārayet |
dakṣiṇa caturtha palla<| ṃ |>vaṃ dhṛtavaṃtāvarī saṃkuṃcya muṣṭi jeṣṭadvayaṃ gāḍheṇa lalāṭasthitaṃ vā caturthapalavaṃ vidhṛta narttāvarī saṃkuṃcya muṣṭitarjanīdvayaṃ sūcīṃ prasārya anyonya ūrdhvavāmapārsve pṛṣṭhāpattata sira prasphu[3]lamukhaṃ bhādhva netraṃ vāmadaṣṭhenāvaṣṭhaptādharoṣṭapuṭaṃ nirīkṣya bhinnabhrū aviparītena m-ullālayet ayam ākṣepamantraḥ oṃ jvalana hūṃ hrīḥ daha dahana hūṃ hrīḥ paca pācana raudrāyudhadhāriṇi hūṃ phaṭ 2 svāhā | ākṣepa mantrasahitaṃ sarvamaṇḍasthāne badhvā sitasairājyasahitaṃ daśadisaṃ yojya bhūmya adhikaṃ vāsayet | tatraivā[4]kṣepaṃ mantrasahitaṃ japa ādyavasāneṣu paṭasya prayojayet prayojitamātre jvālāvatī viṃsati yojanasahasraṃ saptaratnamayībhavanaṃ prakaṃpitā bhavati | sa viṃsatisahasrayakṣiṇyā sahitaṃ śrīghra 2 āgaccha brūhi sādhakena yam icchati taṃ vaktavyaṃ vidyuvikramajvalitaḥ jvalitapraharaṇamahākṣepaḥ
dvau hastau anyonyāṃjalī dvau [5] maṇibandha lalāṭasthāne nyasya dakṣiṇapañcapalavaṃ vidhire narttavarīm ākuṃcya muṣṭi tatraiva bāhu prasāryaḥ dakṣiṇapādobhayajaṃghaikopari karaṇyasya tarjanya vivṛtā narttāvarī vṛttābhinayaṃ <| ma |> mantram ucārya paladhvayet || dakṣiṇavāmapādasthāne cākamya vāmapādaḥ ubhaye cāmaṇḍaliṃ vivṛta narttāvarī saṃkuṃcya muṣṭhi prasāryaḥ [48\verso] bāhuṃ kopari jaṃghasthāne nyasya tāny avivṛtā narttavarīvṛtābhineyai mantram ucāryaḥ pralaṃbayet aṣṭādaśāyudhavivṛtatejogramahākṣepaṃ tatraivākṣepamantraḥ oṃ jvala hūṃ tapa tāpana hūṃ paca pācana hūṃ hana 2 hūṃ daha dāhana hūṃ hrīḥ dhrūṃ raṃ je svāhā || ayaṃ maṃtrasahitaṃ ākṣepaṃ badhvā paṭe vā maṇḍalanyāse vā ekaikāyudhe pūrvokta ā[2]kṣepaṃ mantrasahitaṃ ri yojayet | saha niyojitamātraḥ ekaikāyudhaṃ mantradharaḥ ājñāvidheyo bhaveti ayam evākṣepa mahardhikaṃ mahānubhāva | tatraivākṣepaṃ mantrasahitaṃ badhvā svedāṃbujamahārākṣasyo japābhiyuktaḥ trisaṃdhyaṃ bhagavatyā prayojayet pūrvoktasamayamaṇḍale raudrāhutya preto<| to |>tkaṭa trikoṇa vajraparyanta [3] sabhaṃ trailokyaṃ dāraṇaṃ aṣṭhāśītyā maṇḍala pūrvoktakṣepamaṃtrasahitaṃ badhvā prayojayet caturdisanti veṭyā disādigaṃ digvidikṣuḥ anaṃtāparyaṃtalokadhātu vyavasthāpayati | taṃ siddhiṃ karoti |
dvitīyākṣepaḥ dvau hastau 'nyonya lalāṭe nyasya namaskāraṃ vāmapāda purastākramya dakṣiṇapāda tasya purastākrama dvau aṃjalīvivṛta[4]narttāvarī bhrāmya prasārya dvau hastau mantram uccārya pṛṣṭha<| e |> cālayet | tathaiva saṃmukhadarśana vāma ūrdhaheṣṭhaṃ tatraiva tālaṃ caktravartticakravikramasvāmimahākṣepaṃ tantraiva mantraḥ o<| ṃ |> namo bhagavati hūṃ hūṃ 2 prajvalita hūṃ vara vara he hūṃ <2> hūṃ 2 phaṭ svāhā | ayam ākṣepaṃ mantrasahitaḥ pūrvokta saṃticakra sapta vārāṃ prayojayet | pauṣṭikacakraṃ ta[5]thaiva ca saha prayojitamātreṇākṣararājaṃ aviparītaṃ siddhim āyāti mantradharaḥ ||
anenaiva prakāreṇa ākṣepaṃ badhvāḥ pūrvavajravijṛṃbhanamahāvajraṃ sapta vāraṃ mahākṣepaṃ badhvā nirīkṣayet | dakṣiṇa paścima uttarato traiva prayoktavyaḥ vidyotame mahāmantreṣu yaṃtreṣu vidhinā prayoktavyaḥ ||
caturthākṣepaṃ pravakṣyāmi sthita utkuṭā[49\recto]śane prasārya jaṃghadvayaṃ pādāṃ ūrudvayaṃ anyonya guhyasthāne saṃyojya dvau hastau anyonya padmākārau dakṣiṇaparidakṣiṇajaṃghaṃ nyaset | vāmakopari tatraiva jaṃghaṃ jeṣṭadvayaṃ anyonyakaniṣṭhadvayaṃ tathā jeṣṭadvayena lalāṭe nyaset || mantram ucārya dakṣiṇacaturthapallavaṃ vivṛtā kaniṣṭā narttāyanīmanibandhasthāne kani[2]ṣṭhāvādyasūlamudrā nyaset | punar api maṃtram ucāryaḥ kaniṣṭhā saṃkuṃcya sūlamudrā <| ma |> ūrdha pasārya akṣayam ākṣepamantraḥ oṃ visva visvake śrī hūṃ ṣṭraṃ visva visva hrīḥ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā | vidyuvidyuvaisvāsavikramamahākṣepaṃ
pūrvoktaraudrāhutihuta tatkuṃḍa prathamaṃ ākṣepaṃ badhvā caturviṃśa<| e |> niyojayet | saha niyojita[3]tamātraḥ vaisvānara tatkuṇḍa saṃdigdhā bhavati | tadākṣepaṃ badhvā pretotkaṭamahāmaṇḍale caturdisaṃ niyoja<| te |>yet | trikonamaṇḍalas tathā caturtha mudrāmaṇḍalaṃ vidyur nāma mahāmaṇḍalaṃ vā vidyotame mahāmaṃtre śāntikapauṣṭikābhicāre hunana kuṇḍali a(?)yam ākṣeparāja sapta sapta vārāṃ prayoktavya saha niyoji[4]tamātraḥ asani agni patati vikaṭas tathā
vidyotame mahākṣepa parisamāptaḥ || o ||
athātaḥ bhagavatyā mūlamudrālakṣaṇaṃ bhavati | dvau hastau hastena candanāvaliptaḥ aṃguṣṭhena gopyaḥ mārayet catuthanti ciṭi 2 kāci muktikaraṃ dvau bāhu<| au |> prasārya upalipya tarullityaḥ bhrāmayet || punar api gandhenāvalipya [5] dhūpayet | dvau hastata<| ṃ |>laṃ gandhapuṣpāṃjaliṃ pūrayitvā daśapallavaṃ anyonyaprathama śākyamunis tathāgatanamaskāraṃ gandhapuṣpanivedya<| ṃ |> datvā tatraiva vaktradharaṃ padmadharaṃ tathā paścād vi<| jva |>dyujvālākarārālī svedāṃbujamahārākṣasyā mudrā vajramayī maṃtradhareṇa<| e |> suvyaktena sulakṣaṇaṃ labdhāpadesa vidyotame samayajñaḥ guhyasthāne [49\verso] badha sthirap<| ya |>admāsana paṭasyāgrataḥ bodhicittasamudrāṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ punar api dvau hastau hastena gandhāvaliptaḥ aṃguṣṭāṃguṣṭhena gopya mārayet artti vicartti ciṭi ciṭi kā vimuktikaraṃ || punar api gandhāvalipta dhūpayet || dvau hastatalaṃ sugandhapuṣpagarbhasthitāṃjali daśapalla<| ¯ |>vānyonya nāsāgre jeṣṭadvayaṃ ekasūtreṇā[2]vāryaḥ kaniṣṭādvayaṃ tathākṛti jeṣṭadvayena kaniṣṭadvayāgre tiṣṭet | anāmikādvayanakhakarṇṇa anyonyaṃ tasya pṛṣṭe madhyamasūcīdvayaṃ prasārya dvau nakhanakhakarṇṇa anyonya tasyopari pṛṣṭe tarjanyadvayaṃ prasārya nakhakarṇṇa anyonyatarjanīmadhyamadvayāttaraṃ dvayanaṃtaraṃ viralākṛtimadhyo anāmikādvayāta[3]raṃ tṛtīyāṃgulaṃ viralā tatraivāmukhīkṛtya lalāṭe sthāpya dakṣiṇabāhau vāmabāhau hṛdaye tathā | upasaṃhārya punar api gandhāvaliṣtaḥ pūrvoktavidhinā mudrāṃ badhvā japet | dine 2 ekalakṣaṃ japet || aya mūlamudrā |
tathaiva mudrāṃ badhvā jeṣṭadvayaṃ mūlaparvakaṃ nyasya prādya 2 syasekaniṣṭadvayena āraṃbhitamukta[4]namaskāraṃ ||
ayam āvāhanamudrā | dvau hastau pūrvoktavidhinā saṃskāryaḥ dvau muṣṭiṃ badhvā anyonyajeṣṭadvayena tarjanīdvaye<| aṃ |> pāsvamadhyamaparve nyaset | madhyamāsūcīdvayaṃ prasārya kuṃḍalākṛti || anyonyadakṣiṇakaniṣṭa prasārya jeṣṭadvayena grāhya 2 tarjanyādvayaṃ spaśayet | āraṃbhitavṛtteḥ namaskāraḥ |
ayaṃ [5] preṣamudrā || tatraiva mudrā badhvā madhyamajeṣṭā abhyaṃtaraṃ saṃkuṃcya tarjanyā tarjanīdvayeṃ | anyonyamadhyamasūcīdvayaṃ aṃkusākṛti jeṣṭadvayāya pāsve nyaset | dvayaṃ namaskāraṃ vṛtte |
gandhamudrā dvau hastau anyonyatarjanyadvayaḥ prasārya anyonyajeṣṭamadhyamāsūcīdvayaṃ pṛṣṭe m-aṃkusākṛtim ākramya kaniṣṭadvayaṃ prasārya<ḥ> [50\recto] anyonyakṛtenamaskāraḥ
ayaṃ puṣpamudrā | tathaiva muṣṭiḥ anyonyajeṣṭadvayāṃtajanīdvayam aṃkuśākāraṃ nyaset | madhyamāsūcidvayaṃ prasāryaḥ anyonyakaniṣṭadvayaṃ viralākṛtiṃ bodhaye namaskāraṃ kuruḥ
ayaṃ nivedyamudrā || dvau hastau muṣṭiṃ badhvā vāmamuṣṭyā pra pari dakṣiṇamuṣṭi sthāpya tatraiva jeṣṭa prasāryaḥ vāmajeṣṭa[2]s tathā dvau tarjanyaḥ prasāryaḥ dvau nakha spṛṣṭā anyonyanamaskārāvarttaḥ || dvau hastau anyonyāñjaliṃ jeṣṭadvayābhyantaraṃ sakucya tarjanīdvayaṃ prasārya madhyamasūcīdvayaṃ tarjanīdvayamadhyamaparvaṇyasya dakṣiṇānāsikā vāmakaniṣṭyā anyonya vāma anāmikādakṣiṇakaniṣṭa anyonyanamaskāravṛttaiḥ
ayaṃ pradīpamudrā | dvau hastapṛṣṭau a[3]nyonyavāmakaniṣṭadakṣiṇakaniṣṭapṛṣṭaḥ dakṣiṇānāmikā vāmakaniṣṭā anyonyasūlāgra dakṣiṇajeṣṭakaniṣṭe nyaset || vāma anāmikā dakṣiṇamadhyamasūci anyonya pāsayet | vāmamadhyamasūcī prasārya vāmatarjanī dakṣiṇatarjanyā upari avaṣṭabhya jeṣṭāgre sparśayet | dakṣiṇatarjanī vāmamadhyamāsūcī agraparve nyaset [4] dvitīyanamaskāravṛtteḥ
ayaṃ balimudrā || disaṃ vidisaṃ digbhāgaṃ anaṃtarāgaṃ paryanta aparyaṃtaṃ ayaṃ mudrā mantrasahitaṃ vidyotame mahāmantre sarvamaṇḍalasthāne balinivedyaṃ kuryāt |
vidyujvālākarālīsvedāṃbujamahārākṣasyā mudrāpaṭala samāptaḥ || o ||
dvitīyamudrālakṣaṇan nibodhataḥ prathamaṃ prākāra dvitīyaṃ disābaṃdha tṛ[5]tīyaṃ paṃjaḥ caturtha kīlanaṃ paṃcamaṃ āsanaṃ caiva yathā tathā |
prathamaṃ prākāramudrā hastaṃ hastena gandhāvaliptaṃ sodhayot | aṅguṣṭaṃguṣṭhasandhi dvau hastapṛṣṭaḥ | anyonyakaniṣṭena pāsayet | anāmikādvayaṃ madhyamasūcidvayena tathaiva <| ca |> pāsayet | jeṣṭadvayaṃ tathā ayaṃ disābandhamahāmudrā | ayaṃ mantra | oṃ vegavighna kula 2 hūṃ hūṃ ra hūṃ ra hūṃ [50\verso] bandha bandha sarvadisāṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā || ayaṃ mantra mudrāsahitaṃ sarvamaṇḍalajāpa āhutisthāne dasadisā prayojyayet |
dvau hastau anyonyavāṇibandhaṃ dakṣiṇapañcapallava dakṣiṇe prasārya vāma|pañcapalavaṃ vāmaṃ prasāryaḥ jeṣṭadvayaṃ kaṇiṣṭadvayaṃ ca saṃkucya sirasyante bhrāmayet sīmābandhamuhāmudrā || ayaṃ mantarḥ oṃ śruṃ śrūṃ huṃ hūṃ dhruṃ dhruṃ hana hana daha [2] paca hūṃ mahāsīmā bandhāmi svāhā || iyaṃ mudrā maṃtrasahitaṃ vidyotame mahāmantre sarvamaṇḍala āhutijapasthāne śīmābandhe niyojayet |
tathaiva dvau ha<ḥ>tau mānibandhasthāne anyonyamuṣṭiṃ badhvā dakṣiṇatarjanī dakṣiṇyā prasāryaḥ vāmatarjanī vāmā prasāryaḥ jeṣṭadvayena tajanyadvayamadhyamaparvvani nyasya heṣṭatāluheṣṭagrīvāṃte dvau muṣṭau nyase[3]t | dakṣiṇatarjanī dakṣiṇakarṇṇe anyonyavāmatarjanyā vāmakarṇe tathā maṃntram ucārya ātmano dakṣiṇena bhrāmayet | punar api mantram uccāryaḥ vajramayīprākāraṃ cittayitvā punar api bhrāmayet | saptataḥ aṃyaṃ prākāraṃmudrā mantraḥ oṃ prasphoṭa 2 jvala 2 nāga hūṃ khaḍgāṃgī hūṃ prakvalitaprākāra svāhā | ayaṃ mantra mudrāsahitaṃ tatra pratibaddhaṃ maṇḍalā[4]huti japasthāne pūrvoktamudrāmantrasahitaṃ prakāraṃ niyojayet |

athātaḥ yakṣiṇyā mudrālakṣaṇa bhavati | advau tāvat | hastena hastam caṃdanāvaliptā manibandhasthānaṃ aṃguṣṭānyonyavarttaṃ puṣpaṃ nivedayet paścād guhyasthāne paṭāṃtaṃ pṛṣṭe vā mudrāṃ bandhayet dvau hastau sirasthāne sthāpyaḥ evaṃ dakṣiṇabāhukopari pra[52\verso]sārya dakṣiṇatarjanyā vāmamadhyamasūcī anyonyadakṣiṇakarṇṇe kareṇa dhārayet | vāmapādapurastākrama dakṣiṇapādakāparikā ekasūtraṃ ? dhārayet | ubhayajaṃghakāpari aṃguṣṭāṃgulaṃ dhārayet sarvanetra ūddhvadṛṣṭi vāmapādāṃ nihalayet | ayaṃ vidyuvikrama ākṣepamantraḥ oṃ ṭha ṭha ṭha ṭha ṭha ṭha hūṃ phaṭ svāhā || ayaṃ [2] ākṣepa yakṣiṇyā sarvasthāne prayojayet | dvau hastau anyonyajeṣṭadvayena kaniṣṭhadvayapṛṣṭhe mūlaparva pāsayet || vāmānāmikā dakṣiṇatarjanyā anyonyadakṣiṇyānāmikā vāmatarjanyānyonyamadhyamāsūcīdvayaṃ prasāryaḥ bhinnayavamātraḥ ayaṃ mūlamudrā || tathaiva ca mudrāṃ badhvā jeṣṭadvayam ubhayam ālīḍhayet | ā[3]vāhanaṃ tatraiva dvayam ākramaprākramyāṣṭa<| ¯ |>prālīḍha visarjayenaṃ \ || anenaiva dvau hastau anyonyamadhyamāsūci abhyantara saṃkuṃcya jeṣṭadvayena madhyamāsūcidvaya madhyaparva dakṣiṇatarjanī vāmajeṣṭapṛṣṭe vāmatarjanī dakṣiṇajeṣṭa anāmikāsūcīdvaya anyonyakaniṣṭhadvayena anāmikādvayamadhyamaparvaṇi nyaset | gandhamudrā | [4] dvau hastau pṛṣṭa anyonyakaniṣṭadvayaṃ aṃkuśākucya anāmikāmadhyamāsūcī | tatraiva jeṣṭadvayena anyonyatarjanīdvayaṃ tathaiva ca puṣpamudrā | tatraiva mudrā badhvā tarjanīdvayena jeṣṭadvayāgra spṛset | madhyamāsūcīdvayaṃ anyonya prasāryaḥ ayaṃ nivedanamudrā || dvau muṣṭiṃ badhvā dvau jeṣṭa ubhayakaniṣṭadvaya anyonyasūcyā tarjanīdva[5]dvayaṃ kuṃḍalākṛtiṃ dakṣiṇajeṣṭanatasya madhye kramet | ayaṃ pradīpamudrā | anenaiva prakāreṇa dvau muṣṭiṃ badhvā anyonyajeṣṭadvayena anāmikādvayajeṣṭayāgreṇāpi spṛset | tarjanyadvaānyonya prasārayet || ayaṃ balimudrā gandhamudrā gandhamaṃtrasahitaṃ puṣpamudrā puṣpamaṃtraṃ tathaiva nivedya tathaiva pradīpam ipi tathā [53\recto] dve dve saṃyojyat | namūḍhacetasā siddhim icchati sādhakaḥ iha loke sukhaṃ pranītaṃ bhaya nāsti paratra ca |
yakṣiṇyā mudrāpatalaṃ samāptaḥ || o ||
athātaḥ yakṣiṇyā sādhanamaṇḍalaṃ dvihastaṃ aṣṭakoṇaṃ saptavedikāparivṛtaṃ pūrvoktavastre evam ālikhya garbhamaṇḍale uttarābhimukhaṃ sthāpayitavyaḥ mānuṣāsthima[2]yaṃ kīlaṃ aṣṭāṃgula pañcaraṅgikasūtreṇa veṣṭayet | hṛdayamaṃtreṇa sasyāṣṭaśatābhimaṃtritaṃ kīlasahitaṃ aṣṭakoṇe haṃtavyaḥ || paṭasya samaṃtena kṛṣṇaraktaśrūrṇṇajvālāmālavinirgataṃ darśayet pūrvavat | prathamavedike bālakṛṣṇarūpaḥ dvitīyavedike kṛṣṇupretarūpaḥ tṛtīyavedike aṃkusaṃ caturthavedike vi[3]nāyakaḥ pañcamavedī mahākālaḥ ṣaṣṭavedyāṃ garuvāhanaṃ saptamavedyakāyāṃ indra vajrahastaḥ || etaiḥ samastaiḥ kṛṣṇavarṇai kṛtakarapuṭaiḥ paṭasya pṛṣṭe dakṣiṇavedī kapālaṃ dvitīyavedī siraṃ | tṛtīya khadgaṃ | caturtha saraṃ pañcamavedī kṛṣṇabhujaṃga trayo pāṇinaḥ ṣaṣṭhavedī kūrmaḥ saptavedī yamadaṇḍa || etaiḥ [4] samastaiḥ paṭābandhaṃ bravīt | paścimaprathamadevī vajraḥ dvitīyavedī asupramuktapretaḥ tṛtīyavedī pasupati | caturthavedī yamakadaṇḍaḥ | paṃcavedī pāśaṃ | ṣaṣṭavedī ajagaranāṃ nāśakaḥ saptame vedī kṛṣṇakuṃbhaḥ || paṭasyābhimukhaṃ prathamavedī vajraparaśuṃ dvitīyavedī vajrapāsaṃ tṛtīyavedī vetāḍaṃ caturthavedī va[5]jraṃkusaṃ | pañcamavedī trayo kṛṣṇabhujaṃgāḥ pūrvapāsitaḥ ṣaṣṭhame mayūraḥ saptame hārīti priyaṃkarasahitaṃ etaiḥ samastaiḥ nyāsaiḥ vidhivihitaḥ umūḍhonā nyāsayitavyaḥ
pūrvoktavidhinā gandhapuṣpadhūpabalipradīpamālyopahāraṃ datvā maṇḍala samantena trayo dhārmikasatva mūlamudrāmantrasahitaṃ ātmā[53\verso]naṃ sapta vārā prayojayet | paṭasyābhimukha pūrvoktākṣepaṃ badhvā stabadhvanetra ūrdhvadṛṣṭi paṭaṃ nirīkṣya ekatra cittayet | saha cintitamātra paṭa unnamati | paścime pūrvan namati |
punar api ākṣepaṃ badhvā tathaiva dṛṣṭiṃ paṭasyābhimukhaṃ nirīkṣayet | pūrvapaścima uttara matidṛṣṭyā paścima pūrve paṭasya prātihārya mukhe bhīṣaṇa ba[2]ndhasthiraḥ vaprāsana mūlamantra nayutaṃ japet | japāṃte mudrā bandhā takṣaṇa ceṭī āgatya varaṃ brūhi sādhakaḥ sādhakena na vaktavyaḥ yakṣiṇyā presitā ceṭī kṛtimākṛtimaṃ madhuravacana mudrāt t | yadi kṛkyatrimahāmāmi sādhakaḥ na vaktavyaḥ mātarī ajī prajī bhaginīti m-udīrayet | ceṭī sādhakena yaṃ icchasi karomīti bila[3]m asuravivaraṃ nidhiṃ rasarasāyaṇaṃ sarvasatvarujaṃ vā satvākarṣarṇaṃ sarvacchedanaṃ bhedanaṃ bandhanaṃ tāḍana vidveṣaṣaṇaṃ yatra yatra preṣayasi taṃ gṛhītvā śīghram āgacchāmi yadi visatvādibhi bhūviṣyāmi sapathaṃ kuru bhāyāmita vā sādhakaḥ vidyādhareṇa yakṣiṇyā aparīkṣitaṃ na vaktavyam sūlamudrā maṃtrasahitaṃ sapta vārāṃ nivedayet ṣaṭ mu[4]khamaṃtrasahitaṃ yakṣiṇyā saṃmukhaṃ darśayet | saha darśitamātra kṛtimā ceṭī sadyaḥ patati |

akṛṃtimā yakṣiṇī sādhakasya kṛtarapuṭaḥ triḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sādhakasya purastā tiṣṭanti | sādhakena āsanaṃ datvā dakṣiṇyā sthitaḥ sādhakena puṣpalohamayaṃ aṃkusaṃ svedāṃbujamahārākṣasī hṛdayamaṃntreṇa saptābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā yakṣi[5]ṇyāṣliṣet mahāvasena bandhayet | paścān mantradhareṇa bhaginī bhāryā vā vaktavyā iti | ratnatrayopakārāṃ katum icchāmi bhaginī | sā āha | kīdṛsaṃ vā katum icchati sādhakaḥ sādhakena rasaṃ prayaccha bhaginīti tatkṣaṇo nātikramya yatra sphāṭ rasaṃ gṛhītvā śīghram āgacchati āgacchata svāgataṃ kuru | sādhakena rasaṃ gṛhya ekakarṣā[54\recto]śatāṃ pṛset | ekasahasraṃ kanakaṃ bhavet | ekasahasraṃ dvisahasraṃ dvisahasraṃ ṣasahasraṃ ṣaṭsahasraṃ yāvajīvaṃ ratnatraye avisaṃviditāṃ etadagraṃ ceṭikaṃ upajīvyāmāna yāvataḥ maitrībalaṃ buddho bhagavāṃ utpadyati tāvat sukhaṃ jīvitā dānapāramitā paripūrayanti martyatī parivajanīyaṃ <| | |> yāvatkalpatraya jarārogavimuktaḥ dānaśī[2]lakṣāṃtivīryadhyānaprajñādaśakuśalakarmapathasamanvāgato bodhisatvo bhaviṣyati | bhave bhavāṃtaṃ

iti vidyujvālākarālī svedāṃbujamahārākṣasyā lallāṭanirmitaḥ vidyur nāma yakṣiṇyā sarvaduṣṭanigrahārthaṃ sarvasamaya anugrahārthaṃ yatra yatra preṣyanti sa tatra tatra siddhir bhavati
vidyu nāma yakṣiṇa kalpa samāptaḥ || o ||
athātaḥ [3] dvitīyayakṣiṇya kṣiti ulka nāma mahāyakṣiṇyā pūrvoktapretotkaṭamahāmaṇḍalavidhinā m-ālikhyaḥ vaṭaphalake vā śaphalake vā pūrvoktavidhinā m-ālikhya raktakaravīrapuṣparaktacandanaguguludhūpaṃ dahatā samāptena na gṛhya punar api gandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ datvā sūnyagṛhe vā gahvare vā sravaṃte vā yatra vṛkṣa vṛta udāre vā jinasyārthasthā[4]sthāne m-āsritya bhikṣabhaikṣahāreṇa vā kandamūlaphalāhāreṇa vā ekalakṣaṃ japet japāṃte nāgakāṣṭair agniṃ prajvālya bukapuṣpasarjjamisreṇāṣṭasahasraṃ juyāt | yakṣiṇī siddhā bhavati ||

athāta mudrāmaṃntra pravakṣyāmi punaḥ mantradhareṇa | oṃ vidyuṃ dhruṃ 2 uṭ 2 hūṃ 2 hūṃ 2 sphuṭa sphoṭaya svāhā || ayaṃ mūlamantraḥ oṃ ūrṇṇa ru ūrṇṇa ru hūṃ [5] āgaccha ūrṇṇa ru | svedāṃbujamahārākṣasyājñā mātikramya mama kāryaṃ kuru svāhā || ayam āvāhanamantraḥ | oṃ dhuna 2 dhuna 2 ūrṇṇa śrut || hūṃ gaccha gaccha mahāyakṣiṇī jvālāvatī puṣpāvakīrṇṇa punarāgamanaṃ kuru svāhā | preṣaṇamantraḥ preṣaṇaprasphullasukhaṃ āvāhane niṣpiyamukhaṃ sarvayakṣiṇyā oṃ dhūṃ hūṃ svāhā || gandhamantra oṃ sraṃ [54\verso] jriṃ huṃ hūṃ svāhā || puṣpamantraḥ oṃ kuru kuṃje kuru anyaṃ hūṃ svāhā || nivedyanivedanamantraḥ oṃ khuṃ khuṃ jrīḥ duḥ hūṃ svāhā || dhūpamantraḥ oṃ puṣpa vi hūṃ svāhā || ayaṃ pradīpamantraḥ oṃ ṣuṭ 2 vajrīḥ i hūṃ svāhā || balimantraḥ ||
athātaḥ ākṣepalakṣaṇaṃ bhavati | prathamaṃ ākṣepaṃ dvau hastau anyonya sirāṃte atikramya dvau <| hastau |> muṣṭau badhvā dakṣiṇa[2]muṣṭi tatraiva karṇṇe sthāne tiṣṭet madhyamāsūci prasārya vāmamuṣṭi vāmapāda trayaḥ jaṃghaṃ ghṛṣṭakopari tiṣṭet | ayam ākṣepaṃ badhvā stadhvanetraḥ ūrdvṛdṛṣṭiḥ mantrasahitaṃ yakṣiṇyāgrataḥ bandhayet | oṃ ghraṃ ghruṃ kṣuṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā || ayam ākṣepamantraḥ hastena staṃ gandhāvaliptaṃ ciṭi 2 kā vimuktikara dvau muṣṭau badhvā anyonyamadhyamāsūci[3]dvayaṃ prasārya anyonyatarjanīdvayaṃ jeṣṭadvayāgra aṃkusākṛtiṃ nyaset || caturṣpathe vajrakṛtiḥ | ayaṃ mūlamudrā | dvau tarja saṃkuṃcya tarjanyadvayapṛṣṭe jeṣṭadvayena avaṣṭabhya prāhīṣṭe(?) jeṣṭadvayaṃ āvāhanamudrā || tarjanyadvaya prasārya anyonyamadhūmāsūci abhyaṃtaraṃ saṃkuṃcya jeṣṭadvayānyonyagrāhayā ayaṃ preṣaṇamudrā | tatraiva muṣṭau tarja[4]nyadvayaṃ abhyaṃtara saṃkuṃcya jeṣṭadvayā kaniṣṭadvayaṃ prabodhayet | gandhamudrāḥ tathaiva muṣṭi jeṣṭakaniṣṭābhyantara saṃkuṃcya dakṣiṇatarjanī prasārya vāma anāmikā dakṣiṇatarjanī anyonya prasāryaḥ ayaṃ puṣpamudrā | dvau hastau ubhayāṃjaliṃ kṛtvā jeṣṭadvayena tarjanīdvayamūlaparvaṇi nyaset | dhūpamudrāḥ tatraivāṃjali jeṣṭadvayaḥ [5] aṃkuśākṛtiḥ tarjanīdvayaṃ tathaiva ca ayaṃ nivedyanivedanamudrā || tathaivaṃ jaliṃ jeṣṭadvayena madhyamāsūci madhyamasūcikaṃ nyaset || pradīpamudrā | anenaiva prakāreṇa dvau hastau ubhayāṃjaliṃ kṛtvā jeṣṭadvayaṃ abhyantara saṃkuṃcya kaniṣṭadvayaṃ tathaiva ca balimudrā
pūrvoktayakṣiṇyā sādhayitum icchatā vidyādhareṇa yakṣiṇya<| ¯ |>ḥ paṭaṃ [55\recto] gṛhyaḥ pūrvokta<| vidhinā |> ekatra sthānāsṛtya suvarṇṇagairikena ekahastaṃ catu<| ṣa |>ḥkoṇaṃ niveditaṃ paścimadvāramaṇḍalam ālikhya puṣpalohamayaṃ kīlakaṃ aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇaṃ sarṣapa mūlamantreṇa aṣṭatābhimantr<i>taṃ kīlasahitaṃ catuḥkoṇe hattavyaḥ paścimadvāre agnikuṇḍaḥ pūrvaprathamavedī śrīvatsaṃ tṛtīyena nandyāvartta [2] dakṣ<| e |>iṇaprathamavedī kuṃbhaṃ | dvitīye padmaṃ tṛtīyavedī aṃkusaṃ paścimadvāre vedī vajrapadmasthitaṃ dvitīye pāśaṃ tṛtīye vajraghaṇṭāḥ uttaravedī prathamaṃ vajrakuṃbhaṃ dvitīye haṃlaṃ caturdhe koṇeṣu tṛsu saṃkhaṃ nyaset | dakṣiṇāvartta etaiḥ samastaiḥ suvarṇṇagairikeṇa nyāsayet | pūrvoktaḥ yakṣiṇyā paṭaḥ paścimābhimukhaṃ [3] sthāpyaḥ gandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ datvā pūrvoktākṣepaṃ datvā paṭaṃ nirīkṣayet | pūrvoktavidhinā ekalakṣaṃ japet kṛṣṇapakṣena siddhyati tatparaḥ dine 2 saptāhutiṃ kuryāt | āhuti ante mahāsamājaṃ parivarttam ekaṃ pūrṇṇādgirattaṃ kurvīta pratītyagarbhaṃ sugatasya caityaṃ

sidhyāsannakāle rāgabahulo dveṣabahulā yāyānti mantra[4]dharaḥ rūpaṃ rūpeṇa viraddhā śabdagrahaṇaṃ ras<| y |>a sparśa tathaiva ca | tatkṣaṇā preṣitaṃ ceṭī bho sādha varaṃ brūhi bhayāmi sādhaka | sādhakena pacaviṣayajñānākusena ayatanaṃ ceṭi parīkṣayet | svedāṃbujaṃ mahārākṣasī mūlamudrāṃ badhvā yakṣiṇyā saṃmukhaṃ darśayet yadi kṛttimā ceṭī sā praruditā bhavati | evaṃ pratyeti [5] yady akṛttimā yakṣiṇyā mudrā bhavati sā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya vadati | varaṃ brūhi sādhaka yam icchasi taṃ karomi rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ jaṃbūdvīparājyaṃ aṣṭāśītisahasraṃ antargatasiddhi uttamamadhyamakaniṣṭā yaṃ preṣisi taṃ siddhi kariṣyāmi sādhakena sādhu bhaginīti vaktavyaḥ yāvajīvaṃ yakṣiṇyam upabhuṃjayet | martyā strī pa[55\verso]rivarjayet sujīvitaṃ sulabdhaṃ ihaloke dānapāramitā paripūrayanti | sulabdhaṃ khalu jīvitaṃ hastigavāparacakrabhayaṃ nāsti |

vidyujvālākarālīsvedāṃbujamahārākṣasya pretotkaṭamahāmaṇḍale dvitīyayakṣiṇyā kalpaḥ samāptaḥ || o ||
athātaḥ tṛtīya svetaraudrī nāma vikhyātā raudryā yakṣiṇyā upacāraṃ pravakṣyāmi |
[2] pūrvoktavidhinā yakṣiṇyā m-ālikhya pāribhadrapuṣpaṃ raktacandanaṃ guguladhūpaṃ dahatā haritālena ekahastaṃ trikoṇaṃ ekavedikā uttaradvāraṃ ekamaṇḍalam ālikhet | garbhamaṇḍale yakṣiṇī svetaraudrī nāma sthāpya | pūrvoktagandhapuṣpadhūpaṃ datvā trisu koṇe kapāla rudhirasarṣapacūrṇṇa sthāpayitavyaḥ saha taila kapālasthi[3]taḥ pradīpaṃ trikoṇe sthāpyaḥ caturtha madhyamaṇḍala mānuṣāsthimayaṃ kīla saḍaṃgulapramāṇaṃ anena mantreṇa saptābhimaṃtritaṃ dakṣiṇakoṇe hattavyaḥ paṭasyāgrataḥ śmasānāṃgāreṇa sakrapratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā muktasikhā vṛttasukhā pralaṃbahastaḥ pādāṃ budbudaraktāsu patitayan ātmanena mantreṇa sarṣapāṣṭasatābhimaṃtritaṃ pra[4]tikṛtiṃ hṛdayasthāne hattavyaḥ ayaṃ mantraḥ oṃ sveta mahāsveta svetaraudrī hana 2 paṭa 2 phaṭa iti māreṇa vaktavyaḥ mānuṣāsthimayaṃ kīlakaṃ caturṣu saptāṃgulapramāṇaṃ pūrvoktamantreṇa aṣṭaśatābhimaṃtritaṃ pūrvoktapratikṛtiṃ śatrūṇāmaṃ gṛhya padena karatalam avaṣṭabhyaḥ ekatra kīla hrīḥ phaṭ iti hṛdayasthāne hattavya<ḥ> [5] dvitīyakīlaṃ hrīḥ phaḍ iti vāmahusthāna harttavyaḥ tṛtīyakīla hrīḥ phaḍ iti nāmaṃ gṛhya vāmajaṃgha brahmasthāne hattavyaḥ caturthakīlaṃ hrīḥ phaḍ iti amukaṃ kīlayāmi iti | dakṣiṇajaṃgha brahmasthāne harttavyaḥ saha nitamātraḥ avaṣṭambahastam ulālayati | śa<| ru |>truṃ caturdisaṃ mārāvaṣṭa badhvakīlaṃ bhavati sīghraṃ pratyā[56\recto]nayanaṃ karttavya yadi pratyānayanaṃ necchati sādhakaḥ || dvitīyarātre tasya puruṣasarīre agnidhūpaṃ niṣkramaṇe samaṃtād yojanaśatatrayaḥ mahārī ṣravarttate uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ | tatpratikṛti mṛdgāmayasamanvitaṃ upalipyaḥ pūrvoktapālibhadrapuṣpasahitaṃ mṛta ?omayam ekīkṛtya gulikaṃ kārayet || ekagulikaṃ gṛhya pūrvokta[2]mantreṇa aṣṭasatābhimantritaṃ satrugṛhe dvāramūle hattavya ucchādayati | uddhṛtya mokṣaḥ utpādasonita upodakīmūlaṃ maṃḍūkavasayas tathā pūrvoktagulikāsahitaṃ kṣipet hutāsane api vaṣasahasraṃ tadahyataparyate svetamaṇḍūkacūrṇṇaṃ tat || radhirapuplutaṃ pūrvokta gulikā svetotpāda kīlaḥ ekīkṛtyaḥ āṃgasamālabhya [3] agni praviset | agninā na dahyate | agnitaṃbhanamantra || viṣākarṇṇasya pūrvoktagulikāṃ susumāravṛṣaṇaṃ tathā padmapatrarasaṃ saṃyuktaṃ svasarīrasamālabhya jalaṃ praviset | jalasatvāṃ paśya jalastaṃbhanam uttamaṃ |

svetaraudrīyakṣiṇyā kalpaḥ samāptaḥ || o ||
athāta vidyujvālākarālī svedāṃbujamahārākṣasyāḥ preto[4]tkaṭamahāmaṃḍalarāje pradhuramahāyakṣiṇyā caturthaṃ kalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmi |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇacaturdasyāṃ ahorātroṣito bhūtvā mantradhara pūrvoktapretotkaṭamahāmaṇḍalavidhinā samālikhya yakṣiṇyā kokilākṣapuṣparaktacaṃdanaguguludhūpaṃ dahatāṃ yakṣiṇyā hṛdayamantrasahitaṃ ayutaṃ japet | oṃ pradhura phuṭa huru 2 hūṃ hūṃ phaṭ [5] phaṭ amukaṃ vasam ānaya svāhā | vetralate kanyāpratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā pūrvoktamantrena aṣṭasatābhimantritā nave vetra pratikṛtiṃ amukī kanyā hattavyāḥ saha hanitamātreṇa mahatā rogena gṛhyate<| ¯ |> | na kaścin mocāpayati | kṣīrasarṣapasalilamisreṇa pūrvoktamaṃtreṇāṣṭasatābhimantritaṃ śnāpayet | muktā bhavati | yakṣiṇyā gandhapuṣpa[56\verso]dhūpaṃ dātavyaṃ dakṣiṇahastatale alaktakamayī kanyāpratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā saptasatābhimaṃtritaṃ sarṣapeṇa paṭasyāgrataḥ tatpratibinvaṃ tāḍayet | tatkṣaṇā kanyākarṣitā bhavet dakṣiṇahastatale pūrvokta yakṣiṇya haritālena samālikhya svedāṃbujamahārākṣasyā mūlamantreṇa sarṣapāṣṭasatābhimantritaṃ tāḍayet | puṣpaphala[2]patra anyapānavastra strīpuruṣadārakadārikā vā ākarṣito bhavati | yadi nākarṣayati mūlamudrāṃ badhvā hastatale gharṣayet | tatkṣaṇād āgacchati tam ākarṣato bhavati |
guhyena yakṣiṇīkarma karttavyaḥ yadi prakāsayati yakṣiṇī parāmukhā yāsyate mantradharaḥ ||
